# Atlas — every branch at once

> Generated by `python -I scripts/physlib.py tree` on 2026-11-05. Do not edit. It is the cross-branch digest: for one branch in full, load its codex with the Skill tool (`codex-<NN-field>`); for any node, Read `knowledge/<path>/README.md`; to query the graph, run `python -I scripts/physlib.py connections …`.

**450 cross-branch links · 80 curated concepts · 37 latent cross-branch and 8 within-branch candidates · 8 curated themes**

## Roster

The agents that report to the orchestrator, with their scope.

- `applied-physicist` — Specialist in interdisciplinary and applied physics — biophysics (protein folding, single molecules, membranes, physics of living systems), Earth and climate physics (atmosphere, radiative forcing, oceans, seismology), medical physics (CT, MRI, radiotherapy, PET, ultrasound), chemical physics (spectroscopy, reaction dynamics, quantum chemistry, surfaces), accelerator physics and device physics (transistors, solar cells, quantum sensors, batteries, thermoelectrics)
- `astrophysicist` — Specialist in astrophysics and cosmology — stars and the Sun, supernovae and compact objects, galaxies and AGN, the Milky Way, cosmology (CMB, inflation, dark energy, BAO, Hubble tension, lensing), dark matter (evidence, detection, MOND), high-energy astrophysics (cosmic rays, GRBs, neutrino astronomy, multi-messenger, FRBs) and exoplanets/planetary science
- `classical-physicist` — Specialist in classical mechanics — Newtonian, Lagrangian and Hamiltonian mechanics, rigid bodies, oscillations and waves, nonlinear dynamics and chaos, and continuum/fluid mechanics including turbulence
- `condensed-matter-physicist` — Specialist in condensed matter physics — crystals, band theory, phonons, semiconductors, DFT and transport; superconductivity (BCS, cuprates, nickelates, hydrides, Josephson); magnetism (spintronics, spin liquids, skyrmions, altermagnets); strongly correlated electrons; topological matter; graphene, moiré and 2D materials; soft matter (polymers, liquid crystals, active matter, glasses)
- `connections-analyst` — Cross-branch analyst that sees all 12 branches of physics at once. It finds, tests and explains connections across branches (shared mathematics, common mechanisms, analogies and where they break, dualities, one field's method solving another's problem) and within a branch (between its subfields). It also integrates several specialists' expert reports into one consistent picture
- `derivation-checker` — Verifies physics mathematics — derivations, formulas, numerical answers and order-of-magnitude claims — using dimensional analysis, limiting cases, symmetry checks and sympy/numpy computation
- `em-optics-physicist` — Specialist in electromagnetism and optics — electrostatics/magnetostatics, Maxwell's equations and EM waves, radiation and scattering, classical optics (interference, diffraction, polarization, imaging), lasers, nonlinear/ultrafast optics, integrated photonics and metamaterials
- `foundations-physicist` — Specialist in the shared foundations of physics — mathematical methods, group theory and symmetry, computational physics (numerics, Monte Carlo, tensor networks, ML), measurement and statistics, units and CODATA constants, and the history/philosophy of physics
- `knowledge-curator` — Maintains the branched physics knowledge base — creates and edits node READMEs, adds verified landmark references, files literature reviews, refreshes papers.md, and keeps TREE.md and links consistent
- `nuclear-physicist` — Specialist in nuclear physics — nuclear structure (shell model, chiral EFT forces, ab initio theory, exotic and superheavy nuclei), reactions and decays (alpha, beta, fission, fusion), nucleon structure (PDFs, proton spin and radius, EIC), heavy-ion physics and the quark-gluon plasma, nuclear astrophysics (BBN, r-process, neutron stars) and applied nuclear physics (reactors, dosimetry, isotopes)
- `paper-scout` — Literature specialist that finds, ranks, reads and summarizes real physics papers from arXiv, INSPIRE-HEP and OpenAlex using scripts/physlib.py
- `particle-physicist` — Specialist in particle physics — quantum field theory, the Standard Model (electroweak theory, Higgs, QCD, flavor, CP violation, hadrons), neutrino physics, beyond-the-Standard-Model physics (SUSY, GUTs, axions, dark-matter particles, muon g-2) and collider/experimental physics (LHC, jets, detectors, future colliders)
- `physics-orchestrator` — Top of the physics agent's hierarchy and its main session, not a delegate. It decides which branch expertise a question needs, consults the field specialists (in parallel when several branches are involved), has the connections analyst integrate their reports, verifies, and writes the answer. Launch with `claude --agent physics-orchestrator` or the `agent` setting. Never spawn it as a subagent; a plain session already plays this role via CLAUDE.md
- `plasma-physicist` — Specialist in plasma physics — Debye shielding, particle drifts, Vlasov kinetics and Landau damping, plasma waves and instabilities, magnetohydrodynamics (reconnection, dynamos, MHD turbulence), magnetic confinement fusion (tokamaks, stellarators, ITER), inertial fusion and laser plasmas (NIF, wakefield acceleration, HED physics), space plasmas (solar wind, magnetospheres) and low-temperature plasmas
- `quantum-physicist` — Specialist in quantum physics — QM formalism and methods, foundations and interpretations, Bell nonlocality, atomic/molecular/optical physics (laser cooling, BEC, clocks), quantum optics, quantum information and computing (algorithms, error correction, qubit hardware, QKD), open and many-body quantum dynamics
- `referee` — Adversarial reviewer for draft physics answers, explanations and literature summaries — checks physical correctness, misconceptions, overclaiming, missing caveats, level-appropriateness and that every citation exists and says what is claimed
- `relativist` — Specialist in relativity and gravitation — special relativity, general relativity and its tests, exact solutions, numerical relativity, modified gravity, black holes (Hawking radiation, information paradox, EHT), gravitational waves (LIGO, PTAs, LISA) and quantum gravity (strings, AdS/CFT, loop quantum gravity)
- `statistical-physicist` — Specialist in thermodynamics and statistical mechanics — laws of thermodynamics, ensembles and partition functions, quantum statistics, entropy and information, phase transitions and the renormalization group, non-equilibrium and stochastic thermodynamics, disordered and complex systems, statistical physics of learning

## Field map

Every subfield and topic: title, path under its field, summary, and its first key equation when short.

### 00 Foundations — Mathematics, Computation and Measurement · `foundations-physicist`
The shared toolkit of every branch — mathematical methods, symmetry, computational physics, measurement and statistics, units and constants, and the history and philosophy of physics.

- **Computational physics** `computational-physics` — Numerical methods that solve physics problems beyond analytic reach — ODE/PDE solvers, Monte Carlo, molecular dynamics, tensor networks and machine learning. · $\text{Euler: }y_{n+1}=y_n+hf(y_n)\ (\mathcal O(h)),\qquad \text{RK4: }\mathcal O(h^4),\qquad \sigma_{\bar A}=\sqrt{\frac{2\tau_{\text{int}}}{N}}\,\sigma_A$
  - **Machine learning for physics** `computational-physics/machine-learning-for-physics` — Neural networks as tools for physics — surrogates, physics-informed networks, neural quantum states, symbolic regression and ML-assisted discovery. · $\mathcal L=\frac1N\sum_i|u_\theta(x_i)-u_i|^2+\lambda\frac1M\sum_j|\mathcal N u_\theta\,(x_j)|^2$
  - **Molecular dynamics** `computational-physics/molecular-dynamics` — Simulating many interacting particles by integrating their equations of motion — force fields, thermostats and ab initio MD. · $V_{\text{LJ}}(r)=4\epsilon\left[\left(\frac\sigma r\right)^{12}-\left(\frac\sigma r\right)^6\right]$
  - **Monte Carlo methods** `computational-physics/monte-carlo-methods` — Stochastic sampling for integrals and statistical systems — importance sampling, Metropolis, cluster algorithms, quantum Monte Carlo. · $P_{\text{acc}}(x\to x')=\min\!\left(1,e^{-\beta[E(x')-E(x)]}\right)$
  - **Numerical methods for ODEs and PDEs** `computational-physics/numerical-methods-for-odes-and-pdes` — Time integrators, symplectic methods, finite differences, finite elements, finite volumes and spectral methods. · $x_{n+1}=x_n+v_n\Delta t+\tfrac12a_n\Delta t^2,\quad v_{n+1}=v_n+\tfrac12(a_n+a_{n+1})\Delta t\ (\text{velocity Verlet})$
  - **Tensor network methods** `computational-physics/tensor-network-methods` — Compressing quantum many-body states using their entanglement structure — MPS, DMRG, PEPS, MERA and tensor-network simulation of circuits. · $|\psi\rangle=\sum_{\{s\}}\mathrm{Tr}\left(A^{s_1}A^{s_2}\cdots A^{s_N}\right)|s_1s_2\cdots s_N\rangle,\qquad S\le\log\chi$
- **History and philosophy of physics** `history-and-philosophy-of-physics` — How physics developed, what its theories say about reality, how explanations and evidence work in physics, and how physics is best learned.
  - **History of physics** `history-and-philosophy-of-physics/history-of-physics` — The development of physical ideas and institutions — the scientific revolution, classical physics, the quantum and relativity revolutions, big science.
  - **Philosophy of physics** `history-and-philosophy-of-physics/philosophy-of-physics` — Conceptual foundations — interpretation of quantum mechanics, the nature of space and time, probability in physics, laws, reduction and emergence.
  - **Physics education research** `history-and-philosophy-of-physics/physics-education-research` — Evidence on how physics is learned — misconceptions, concept inventories, active learning and problem-solving expertise. · $\langle g\rangle=\frac{\%\text{post}-\%\text{pre}}{100-\%\text{pre}}\quad(\text{Hake's normalized gain})$
- **Mathematical methods** `mathematical-methods` — The mathematics physicists use daily — linear algebra and Hilbert spaces, differential equations and Green's functions, complex analysis and special functions, tensors and differential geometry. · $\mathcal L G(\mathbf x,\mathbf x')=\delta(\mathbf x-\mathbf x'),\qquad u(\mathbf x)=\int G(\mathbf x,\mathbf x')f(\mathbf x')\,d\mathbf x'$
  - **Complex analysis and special functions** `mathematical-methods/complex-analysis-and-special-functions` — Analytic functions, contour integration, dispersion relations, asymptotic methods and the special functions of mathematical physics. · $\Gamma(z+1)=z\Gamma(z),\quad n!\approx\sqrt{2\pi n}\left(\frac ne\right)^n,\qquad \int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{-ax^2}dx=\sqrt{\pi/a}$
  - **Differential equations and Green's functions** `mathematical-methods/differential-equations-and-greens-functions` — Ordinary and partial differential equations of physics, separation of variables, Sturm–Liouville theory and Green's-function solutions.
  - **Linear algebra and Hilbert spaces** `mathematical-methods/linear-algebra-and-hilbert-spaces` — Vector spaces, operators, eigenvalue problems, the spectral theorem and infinite-dimensional function spaces. · $A=\sum_n\lambda_n|n\rangle\langle n|,\qquad \sum_n|n\rangle\langle n|=\mathbb 1,\qquad \dim(\mathcal H_A\otimes\mathcal H_B)=\dim\mathcal H_A\cdot\dim\mathcal H_B$
  - **Tensors and differential geometry** `mathematical-methods/tensors-and-differential-geometry` — Index notation, manifolds, metrics, connections, curvature and differential forms — the language of relativity and gauge theory.
- **Measurement and data analysis** `measurement-and-data-analysis` — How physical quantities are measured and inferred — uncertainty propagation, statistical inference, detectors and instrumentation.
  - **Detectors and instrumentation** `measurement-and-data-analysis/detectors-and-instrumentation` — Sensors, signal chains and noise — photodetectors, cryogenic sensors, amplifiers, lock-in detection and the fundamental noise limits. · $\langle V_n^2\rangle=4k_BTR\,\Delta f,\qquad \langle I_n^2\rangle=2qI\,\Delta f$
  - **Statistical inference in physics** `measurement-and-data-analysis/statistical-inference-in-physics` — Frequentist and Bayesian methods for parameter estimation, confidence intervals, hypothesis testing and discovery claims. · $p(\theta|D)=\frac{p(D|\theta)p(\theta)}{p(D)},\qquad \mathrm{Var}(\hat\theta)\ge\frac{1}{I(\theta)},\qquad Z=\sqrt{q_0}$
  - **Uncertainty and error propagation** `measurement-and-data-analysis/uncertainty-and-error-propagation` — Quantifying and combining measurement uncertainties — statistical vs. systematic, the GUM framework, correlations and significant figures.
- **Symmetry and group theory** `symmetry-and-group-theory` — Symmetry as the organizing principle of physics — Lie groups and representations, Noether's theorem, discrete symmetries, spontaneous symmetry breaking. · $\partial_\mu j^\mu=0,\quad j^\mu=\frac{\partial\mathcal L}{\partial(\partial_\mu\phi)}\delta\phi-K^\mu,\qquad [T^a,T^b]=if^{abc}T^c$
  - **Discrete symmetries and CPT** `symmetry-and-group-theory/discrete-symmetries-and-cpt` — Parity, time reversal and charge conjugation, their violation by the weak interaction, and the CPT theorem. · $P:\ \mathbf x\to-\mathbf x,\quad T:\ t\to-t\ (\text{antiunitary}),\qquad \Theta^2=(-1)^{2s}$
  - **Lie groups and representations** `symmetry-and-group-theory/lie-groups-and-representations` — Continuous symmetry groups, their algebras and representations — SO(3), SU(2), SU(3), Lorentz and Poincaré. · $\mathbf 3\otimes\bar{\mathbf 3}=\mathbf 8\oplus\mathbf 1,\qquad \mathbf 3\otimes\mathbf 3\otimes\mathbf 3=\mathbf{10}\oplus\mathbf 8\oplus\mathbf 8\oplus\mathbf 1$
  - **Noether's theorem and conservation laws** `symmetry-and-group-theory/noethers-theorem-and-conservation-laws` — Every continuous symmetry of the action yields a conserved current — energy, momentum, angular momentum and charge. · $Q=\int d^3x\,j^0,\qquad \frac{dQ}{dt}=0;\qquad T^{\mu\nu}=\frac{\partial\mathcal L}{\partial(\partial_\mu\phi)}\partial^\nu\phi-\eta^{\mu\nu}\mathcal L$
  - **Spontaneous symmetry breaking** `symmetry-and-group-theory/spontaneous-symmetry-breaking` — Ground states that break symmetries of the laws — order parameters, Goldstone bosons, the Higgs mechanism and emergent order. · $V(\phi)=-\mu^2|\phi|^2+\lambda|\phi|^4\Rightarrow|\langle\phi\rangle|=\sqrt{\mu^2/2\lambda}$
- **Units, constants and dimensional analysis** `units-and-constants` — The SI system and its 2019 redefinition, CODATA fundamental constants, dimensional analysis and scaling, natural and Planck units.
  - **Dimensional analysis and scaling** `units-and-constants/dimensional-analysis-and-scaling` — Using units to check, derive and estimate — Buckingham π theorem, similarity, scaling laws and order-of-magnitude reasoning. · $Re=\frac{\rho vL}{\mu},\qquad R(t)\approx\left(\frac{Et^2}{\rho}\right)^{1/5}\ (\text{Taylor–Sedov blast wave})$
  - **Fundamental constants** `units-and-constants/fundamental-constants` — Measured constants of nature — CODATA adjustments, the fine-structure constant, G, and searches for their variation. · $\alpha=\frac{e^2}{4\pi\epsilon_0\hbar c},\qquad R_\infty=\frac{\alpha^2m_ec}{2h}$
  - **Natural units and the Planck scale** `units-and-constants/natural-units-and-planck-scale` — Unit systems that set fundamental constants to one — particle-physics, geometrized, atomic and Planck units — and how to convert back.
  - **SI units and the 2019 redefinition** `units-and-constants/si-units-and-2019-redefinition` — How the kilogram, ampere, kelvin and mole were redefined in terms of exact constants, and how the units are realized. · $mgv=UI\ (\text{Kibble balance}),\qquad K_J=\frac{2e}{h}\approx483\,597.8\ \text{GHz/V},\qquad R_K=\frac{h}{e^2}\approx25\,812.807\ \Omega$

### 01 Classical Mechanics · `classical-physicist`
The motion of particles, rigid bodies and continua under forces — Newtonian and analytical mechanics, oscillations and waves, nonlinear dynamics and chaos, fluids and elasticity.

- **Analytical mechanics** `analytical-mechanics` — Lagrangian and Hamiltonian reformulations of mechanics — generalized coordinates, constraints, phase space, canonical transformations and variational principles.
  - **Canonical transformations and Hamilton–Jacobi theory** `analytical-mechanics/canonical-transformations-and-hamilton-jacobi` — Changes of phase-space variables that preserve Hamilton's equations, generating functions, action–angle variables and the Hamilton–Jacobi equation. · $J=\oint p\,dq,\qquad \omega=\frac{\partial H}{\partial J},\qquad \theta(t)=\omega t+\theta_0$
  - **Hamiltonian mechanics** `analytical-mechanics/hamiltonian-mechanics` — Phase-space dynamics — Hamilton's equations, Poisson brackets, Liouville's theorem and symplectic structure. · $\frac{df}{dt}=\{f,H\}+\frac{\partial f}{\partial t},\qquad \frac{\partial\rho}{\partial t}+\{\rho,H\}=0$
  - **Lagrangian mechanics** `analytical-mechanics/lagrangian-mechanics` — Equations of motion from L = T − V in generalized coordinates, holonomic constraints, Lagrange multipliers and cyclic coordinates. · $\frac{d}{dt}\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot q_k}-\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_k}=\sum_j\lambda_j\frac{\partial f_j}{\partial q_k}\quad(\text{constraints }f_j(q)=0)$
  - **Variational principles** `analytical-mechanics/variational-principles` — Least action, Fermat's principle, the calculus of variations and their role across physics. · $\delta\int_{t_1}^{t_2}L(q,\dot q,t)\,dt=0,\qquad \delta\int n(\mathbf r)\,ds=0\ (\text{Fermat})$
- **Continuum and fluid mechanics** `continuum-and-fluid-mechanics` — Deformable solids and flowing fluids as continua — elasticity, ideal and viscous flow, turbulence, instabilities and convection, low-Reynolds-number flow. · $\rho\left(\frac{\partial\mathbf u}{\partial t}+\mathbf u\cdot\nabla\mathbf u\right)=-\nabla p+\mu\nabla^2\mathbf u+\mathbf f,\qquad \nabla\cdot\mathbf u=0,\qquad Re=\frac{UL}{\nu}$
  - **Elasticity** `continuum-and-fluid-mechanics/elasticity` — Stress, strain and elastic response of solids — Hooke's law, elastic moduli, elastic waves, beams, plates and buckling. · $\sigma_{ij}=\lambda\,\epsilon_{kk}\delta_{ij}+2\mu\,\epsilon_{ij},\qquad v_P=\sqrt{\frac{\lambda+2\mu}{\rho}},\ v_S=\sqrt{\frac\mu\rho},\qquad P_{\text{cr}}=\frac{\pi^2EI}{(KL)^2}$
  - **Ideal and viscous flow** `continuum-and-fluid-mechanics/ideal-and-viscous-flow` — Inviscid and viscous fluid dynamics — Euler and Navier–Stokes equations, Bernoulli, vorticity, boundary layers, lift and drag. · $p+\tfrac12\rho v^2+\rho gz=\text{const},\qquad Q=\frac{\pi R^4\Delta p}{8\mu L},\qquad \delta(x)\approx5\sqrt{\frac{\nu x}{U}}$
  - **Instabilities and convection** `continuum-and-fluid-mechanics/instabilities-and-convection` — How fluid flows become unstable — Rayleigh–Bénard convection, Kelvin–Helmholtz, Rayleigh–Taylor and Plateau–Rayleigh instabilities. · $Ra=\frac{g\beta\Delta TL^3}{\nu\kappa},\qquad \sigma_{\text{RT}}=\sqrt{Agk},\ A=\frac{\rho_h-\rho_l}{\rho_h+\rho_l}$
  - **Low-Reynolds-number flow and microfluidics** `continuum-and-fluid-mechanics/low-reynolds-number-and-microfluidics` — Viscosity-dominated flow — Stokes flow, the scallop theorem and microswimmers, and fluid physics at the micro- and nanoscale. · $\mu\nabla^2\mathbf u=\nabla p,\qquad F_{\text{Stokes}}=6\pi\mu aU$
  - **Turbulence** `continuum-and-fluid-mechanics/turbulence` — Chaotic, multiscale fluid motion — the energy cascade, Kolmogorov scaling, intermittency, wall turbulence and turbulence modelling. · $E(k)=C_K\epsilon^{2/3}k^{-5/3},\qquad \frac{L}{\eta}\sim Re^{3/4},\qquad \frac{\bar u}{u_\tau}=\frac1\kappa\ln\frac{yu_\tau}{\nu}+B$
- **Newtonian mechanics** `newtonian-mechanics` — Motion under forces — Newton's laws, momentum and energy conservation, central forces and orbits, non-inertial frames, and few-body problems. · $\mathbf F=\frac{d\mathbf p}{dt},\qquad E=\tfrac12\mu\dot r^2+\frac{L^2}{2\mu r^2}+V(r),\qquad r(\theta)=\frac{a(1-e^2)}{1+e\cos\theta},\quad T^2=\frac{4\pi^2a^3}{G(m_1+m_2)}$
  - **Central forces and orbits** `newtonian-mechanics/central-forces-and-orbits` — Motion in central potentials — Kepler orbits, effective potential, orbital stability, precession and Rutherford scattering. · $\frac{d^2u}{d\theta^2}+u=-\frac{\mu}{L^2u^2}F(1/u),\ u=\frac1r;\qquad \frac{d\sigma}{d\Omega}=\left(\frac{kq_1q_2}{4E}\right)^2\frac{1}{\sin^4(\theta/2)}$
  - **Conservation laws** `newtonian-mechanics/conservation-laws` — Energy, linear momentum and angular momentum conservation; elastic and inelastic collisions; centre-of-mass frame. · $W=\Delta T,\qquad \boldsymbol\tau=\frac{d\mathbf L}{dt},\qquad v_1'=\frac{m_1-m_2}{m_1+m_2}v_1\ (\text{1D elastic, target at rest})$
  - **Few-body and celestial mechanics** `newtonian-mechanics/few-body-and-celestial-mechanics` — The three-body problem, Lagrange points, orbital resonances, perturbation theory and N-body integration. · $C_J=n^2(x^2+y^2)+2\left(\frac{\mu_1}{r_1}+\frac{\mu_2}{r_2}\right)-(\dot x^2+\dot y^2+\dot z^2)$
  - **Kinematics and Newton's laws** `newtonian-mechanics/kinematics-and-newtons-laws` — Describing motion and relating it to forces — inertial frames, Newton's three laws, friction, drag and free-body analysis. · $\mathbf a=(\ddot r-r\dot\theta^2)\hat{\mathbf r}+(r\ddot\theta+2\dot r\dot\theta)\hat{\boldsymbol\theta},\qquad \Delta v=v_e\ln\frac{m_0}{m_f}\ (\text{rocket})$
  - **Non-inertial frames** `newtonian-mechanics/non-inertial-frames` — Mechanics in accelerating and rotating frames — fictitious forces, Coriolis effects, Foucault pendulum and tides.
- **Nonlinear dynamics and chaos** `nonlinear-dynamics-and-chaos` — Dynamical systems beyond linearity — deterministic chaos, bifurcations, KAM theory, solitons, synchronization and pattern formation. · $\dot x=\sigma(y-x),\ \dot y=x(\rho-z)-y,\ \dot z=xy-\beta z;\qquad x_{n+1}=rx_n(1-x_n);\qquad \lambda=\lim_{t\to\infty}\frac1t\ln\frac{|\delta\mathbf x(t)|}{|\delta\mathbf x_0|}$
  - **Bifurcations and routes to chaos** `nonlinear-dynamics-and-chaos/bifurcations-and-routes-to-chaos` — Qualitative changes in dynamics as parameters vary — saddle-node, pitchfork and Hopf bifurcations, period doubling and Feigenbaum universality. · $\delta=\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{r_n-r_{n-1}}{r_{n+1}-r_n}=4.6692\ldots,\qquad \alpha=2.5029\ldots$
  - **Deterministic chaos** `nonlinear-dynamics-and-chaos/deterministic-chaos` — Sensitive dependence on initial conditions in deterministic systems — Lyapunov exponents, strange attractors and limits of prediction. · $t_{\text{pred}}\approx\frac{1}{\lambda_{\max}}\ln\frac{\Delta_{\text{tol}}}{\delta_0},\qquad D_{KY}=k+\frac{\sum_{i\le k}\lambda_i}{|\lambda_{k+1}|}$
  - **KAM theory and Hamiltonian chaos** `nonlinear-dynamics-and-chaos/kam-theory-and-hamiltonian-chaos` — The survival of invariant tori under perturbation, mixed phase spaces, resonances and Arnold diffusion in conservative systems. · $p_{n+1}=p_n+K\sin\theta_n,\ \theta_{n+1}=\theta_n+p_{n+1}\ (\text{standard map; global chaos for }K\gtrsim0.97)$
  - **Solitons and integrable systems** `nonlinear-dynamics-and-chaos/solitons-and-integrable-systems` — Localized waves that survive collisions — KdV and nonlinear Schrödinger solitons, inverse scattering, Lax pairs and integrability. · $u_t+6uu_x+u_{xxx}=0,\qquad u(x,t)=\frac c2\,\mathrm{sech}^2\!\left[\frac{\sqrt c}{2}(x-ct-x_0)\right]$
  - **Synchronization and pattern formation** `nonlinear-dynamics-and-chaos/synchronization-and-pattern-formation` — Collective rhythms and spatial order out of equilibrium — Kuramoto synchronization, Turing patterns, reaction–diffusion and convection patterns. · $\dot\theta_i=\omega_i+\frac KN\sum_j\sin(\theta_j-\theta_i),\qquad re^{i\psi}=\frac1N\sum_je^{i\theta_j}$
- **Oscillations and waves** `oscillations-and-waves` — Harmonic, damped and driven oscillators, coupled oscillators and normal modes, mechanical waves and acoustics, parametric and nonlinear oscillations.
  - **Coupled oscillators and normal modes** `oscillations-and-waves/coupled-oscillators-and-normal-modes` — Systems of coupled oscillators, normal-mode analysis, beats, and the transition to waves in chains. · $\omega(k)=2\sqrt{\frac{K}{m}}\left|\sin\frac{ka}{2}\right|\quad(\text{monatomic chain})$
  - **Harmonic and damped oscillators** `oscillations-and-waves/harmonic-and-damped-oscillators` — Simple harmonic motion, damping regimes, driven oscillators, resonance and the quality factor. · $x(t)=Ae^{-\gamma t}\cos(\omega_dt+\phi),\ \omega_d=\sqrt{\omega_0^2-\gamma^2};\qquad T_{\text{pend}}\approx2\pi\sqrt{\frac Lg}\left(1+\frac{\theta_0^2}{16}\right)$
  - **Mechanical waves and acoustics** `oscillations-and-waves/mechanical-waves-and-acoustics` — Waves on strings and in media, sound, dispersion, impedance, reflection and transmission, Doppler effect and acoustic phenomena. · $v_{\text{sound}}=\sqrt{\frac{\gamma RT}{M}},\qquad R=\left(\frac{Z_2-Z_1}{Z_2+Z_1}\right)^2,\qquad f'=f\frac{v+v_o}{v-v_s}$
  - **Parametric and nonlinear oscillations** `oscillations-and-waves/parametric-and-nonlinear-oscillations` — Oscillators with time-varying parameters or nonlinear restoring forces — Mathieu equation, Kapitza pendulum, Duffing and Van der Pol oscillators. · $\ddot x+\omega_0^2[1+h\cos(2\omega_0t)]x=0,\qquad \ddot x-\mu(1-x^2)\dot x+x=0\ (\text{Van der Pol})$
- **Rigid-body dynamics** `rigid-body-dynamics` — Rotation of extended bodies — inertia tensor, Euler's equations, torque-free and heavy tops, gyroscopes and precession. · $\mathbf L=\mathsf I\boldsymbol\omega,\qquad I_{ij}=\int\rho\,(r^2\delta_{ij}-x_ix_j)\,dV,\qquad I_1\dot\omega_1-(I_2-I_3)\omega_2\omega_3=\tau_1$
  - **Euler's equations and tops** `rigid-body-dynamics/euler-equations-and-tops` — Body-frame equations of rotation, torque-free motion, the intermediate-axis instability and the heavy symmetric top. · $\Omega_{\text{free}}=\frac{I_3-I_1}{I_1}\omega_3,\qquad \Omega_{\text{prec}}\approx\frac{Mg\ell}{I_3\omega_3}\ (\text{fast top})$
  - **Gyroscopes and precession** `rigid-body-dynamics/gyroscopes-and-precession` — Gyroscopic stability, precession and nutation in technology and nature — from bicycles to the precession of Earth's axis. · $\boldsymbol\tau=\boldsymbol\Omega\times\mathbf L,\qquad \Omega_{\text{geodetic}}=\frac32\frac{(GM)^{3/2}}{c^2r^{5/2}}$
  - **Rotational dynamics and the inertia tensor** `rigid-body-dynamics/rotational-dynamics-and-inertia-tensor` — Moments and products of inertia, principal axes, rolling motion and rotational kinetic energy. · $T=\tfrac12\boldsymbol\omega^{T}\mathsf I\boldsymbol\omega,\qquad I=I_{\text{cm}}+Md^2,\qquad a_{\text{roll}}=\frac{g\sin\theta}{1+I/MR^2}$

### 02 Electromagnetism and Optics · `em-optics-physicist`
Electric and magnetic fields, Maxwell's equations, electromagnetic radiation and scattering, classical optics, and modern photonics and lasers.

- **Classical optics** `classical-optics` — Light as rays and waves — geometric optics, interference and diffraction, polarization, Fourier optics and imaging, coherence. · $n_1\sin\theta_1=n_2\sin\theta_2,\qquad \frac1{s_o}+\frac1{s_i}=\frac1f,\qquad \theta_{\min}\approx1.22\frac{\lambda}{D},\qquad d\sin\theta=m\lambda$
  - **Coherence and statistical optics** `classical-optics/coherence-and-statistical-optics` — Temporal and spatial coherence, the Wiener–Khinchin and van Cittert–Zernike theorems, speckle and intensity interferometry. · $S(\omega)=\int g^{(1)}(\tau)e^{i\omega\tau}d\tau,\qquad \ell_c=c\,\tau_c\approx\frac{\lambda^2}{\Delta\lambda}$
  - **Fourier optics and imaging** `classical-optics/fourier-optics-and-imaging` — Imaging as spatial-frequency filtering — transfer functions, the diffraction limit, holography, phase imaging and super-resolution microscopy. · $U_f(u,v)\propto\iint U_0(x,y)\,e^{-i\frac{2\pi}{\lambda f}(ux+vy)}dx\,dy,\qquad d_{\text{Abbe}}=\frac{\lambda}{2n\sin\alpha}$

- **Electrodynamics** `electrodynamics` — Time-dependent fields — Maxwell's equations, electromagnetic waves and waveguides, potentials and gauge invariance, and the relativistic formulation.
  - **Electromagnetic waves and waveguides** `electrodynamics/electromagnetic-waves-and-waveguides` — Plane waves, polarization, reflection and refraction (Fresnel), skin depth, waveguide modes, resonant cavities and transmission lines.

  - **Potentials and gauge invariance** `electrodynamics/potentials-and-gauge-invariance` — Scalar and vector potentials, gauge transformations, Coulomb and Lorenz gauges, retarded potentials and the physical role of potentials.
  - **Relativistic electrodynamics** `electrodynamics/relativistic-electrodynamics` — Covariant formulation of electromagnetism — the field-strength tensor, four-current, field transformations and invariants.
- **Electrostatics and magnetostatics** `electrostatics-and-magnetostatics` — Static electric and magnetic fields — Coulomb and Gauss's laws, Poisson's equation and boundary-value problems, multipole expansions, Biot–Savart, and fields in matter.
  - **Electrostatics and boundary-value problems** `electrostatics-and-magnetostatics/electrostatics-and-boundary-value-problems` — Solving for static fields — Gauss's law, Poisson/Laplace equations, uniqueness, method of images, separation of variables, conductors and capacitance.
  - **Fields in matter** `electrostatics-and-magnetostatics/fields-in-matter` — Polarization, magnetization, bound charges and currents, the auxiliary fields D and H, dielectric and magnetic response, boundary conditions.
  - **Magnetostatics** `electrostatics-and-magnetostatics/magnetostatics` — Magnetic fields of steady currents — Biot–Savart and Ampère's laws, the vector potential, magnetic dipoles and forces on currents.
  - **Multipole expansions** `electrostatics-and-magnetostatics/multipole-expansions` — Expanding fields of localized sources in monopole, dipole, quadrupole and higher terms; spherical harmonics and their physical applications.

  - **Integrated and fiber photonics** `photonics-and-lasers/integrated-and-fiber-photonics` — Guiding light in fibres and on chips — optical fibres, silicon and lithium-niobate photonics, microresonators and Kerr frequency combs. · $V=\frac{2\pi a}{\lambda}\mathrm{NA}<2.405\ (\text{single-mode}),\qquad \mathrm{NA}=\sqrt{n_1^2-n_2^2},\qquad D=-\frac{\lambda}{c}\frac{d^2n}{d\lambda^2}$

  - **Metamaterials and plasmonics** `photonics-and-lasers/metamaterials-and-plasmonics` — Engineered electromagnetic media — negative index, cloaking, metasurfaces, surface plasmon polaritons and photonic crystals. · $k_{\text{SPP}}=\frac\omega c\sqrt{\frac{\epsilon_m\epsilon_d}{\epsilon_m+\epsilon_d}},\qquad n<0\ \text{when}\ \epsilon<0,\ \mu<0$

### 03 Thermodynamics and Statistical Mechanics · `statistical-physicist`
Heat, work and entropy; how macroscopic behaviour emerges from microscopic statistics; phase transitions and critical phenomena; non-equilibrium and complex systems.

- **Classical thermodynamics** `classical-thermodynamics` — The laws of thermodynamics, thermodynamic potentials and Maxwell relations, heat engines and refrigerators, phase equilibria. · $dU=T\,dS-p\,dV+\mu\,dN,\qquad \Delta S_{\text{universe}}\ge0,\qquad \eta_{\text{Carnot}}=1-\frac{T_c}{T_h}$

  - **Laws of thermodynamics** `classical-thermodynamics/laws-of-thermodynamics` — Temperature, energy conservation, entropy and the second law, and the unattainability of absolute zero. · $\Delta U=Q-W,\qquad dS=\frac{\delta Q_{\text{rev}}}{T},\qquad \oint\frac{\delta Q}{T}\le0$
  - **Phase equilibria and chemical potential** `classical-thermodynamics/phase-equilibria-and-chemical-potential` — Coexistence of phases, the chemical potential, Gibbs phase rule, Clausius–Clapeyron, phase diagrams, mixtures and chemical equilibrium. · $\frac{dp}{dT}=\frac{L}{T\Delta v},\qquad F=C-P+2,\qquad \left(p+\frac{an^2}{V^2}\right)(V-nb)=nRT$
  - **Thermodynamic potentials and Maxwell relations** `classical-thermodynamics/thermodynamic-potentials-and-maxwell-relations` — Internal energy, enthalpy, Helmholtz and Gibbs free energies, Legendre transforms, Maxwell relations and response functions. · $\left(\frac{\partial S}{\partial V}\right)_T=\left(\frac{\partial p}{\partial T}\right)_V,\qquad C_p-C_V=\frac{TV\alpha^2}{\kappa_T}$

  - **Complex networks** `disordered-and-complex-systems/complex-networks` — Statistical physics of networks — random graphs, small worlds, scale-free degree distributions, robustness and spreading processes. · $P(k)\sim k^{-\gamma},\qquad \lambda_c=\frac{\langle k\rangle}{\langle k^2\rangle}$
  - **Localization and disorder** `disordered-and-complex-systems/localization-and-disorder` — How disorder stops transport — Anderson localization, scaling theory, weak localization, and many-body localization in interacting systems. · $H=\sum_i\epsilon_ic_i^\dagger c_i-t\sum_{\langle ij\rangle}(c_i^\dagger c_j+\text{h.c.}),\ \epsilon_i\in[-W/2,W/2];\qquad \psi(r)\sim e^{-r/\xi}$
  - **Percolation** `disordered-and-complex-systems/percolation` — Geometric phase transitions in random connectivity — percolation thresholds, cluster statistics, fractal geometry and applications. · $P_\infty\sim(p-p_c)^\beta,\quad \xi\sim|p-p_c|^{-\nu},\qquad \beta_{2D}=\tfrac{5}{36},\ \nu_{2D}=\tfrac43,\ d_f=\tfrac{91}{48}$
  - **Spin glasses** `disordered-and-complex-systems/spin-glasses` — Magnets with random, frustrated interactions — Edwards–Anderson and SK models, replica symmetry breaking, aging and rugged energy landscapes. · $q_{EA}=\overline{\langle s_i\rangle^2},\qquad T_c^{\text{SK}}=J/k_B$
  - **Statistical physics of learning** `disordered-and-complex-systems/statistical-physics-of-learning` — Neural networks and inference through statistical mechanics — Hopfield networks, Boltzmann machines, perceptron capacity, and theory of deep learning. · $E=-\frac12\sum_{ij}w_{ij}s_is_j,\ w_{ij}=\frac1N\sum_\mu\xi_i^\mu\xi_j^\mu;\qquad \alpha_c^{\text{Hopfield}}\approx0.138,\ \alpha_c^{\text{perceptron}}=2$
- **Equilibrium statistical mechanics** `equilibrium-statistical-mechanics` — Deriving thermodynamics from microscopic states — ensembles, partition functions, quantum statistics, entropy and information, exactly solvable models. · $Z=\sum_ie^{-\beta E_i},\quad F=-k_BT\ln Z,\quad \langle E\rangle=-\frac{\partial\ln Z}{\partial\beta},\quad \langle n_k\rangle=\frac{1}{e^{\beta(\epsilon_k-\mu)}\mp1}$
  - **Ensembles and partition functions** `equilibrium-statistical-mechanics/ensembles-and-partition-functions` — Microcanonical, canonical and grand canonical ensembles, partition functions, equipartition and ensemble equivalence. · $Z_{\text{ideal}}=\frac{1}{N!}\left(\frac{V}{\lambda_{\text{th}}^3}\right)^N,\ \lambda_{\text{th}}=\frac{h}{\sqrt{2\pi mk_BT}};\qquad \mathcal Z=\sum_Ne^{\beta\mu N}Z_N$
  - **Entropy and information** `equilibrium-statistical-mechanics/entropy-and-information` — Boltzmann, Gibbs and Shannon entropy, maximum-entropy inference, Maxwell's demon, Landauer's principle and the thermodynamics of computation. · $H=-\sum_ip_i\log_2p_i,\qquad W_{\text{erase}}\ge k_BT\ln2\approx2.9\times10^{-21}\ \text{J at }300\ \text{K}$

- **Non-equilibrium statistical mechanics** `non-equilibrium-statistical-mechanics` — Systems out of equilibrium — kinetic theory, linear response and fluctuation–dissipation, stochastic dynamics, fluctuation theorems and driven steady states.
  - **Driven systems and non-equilibrium steady states** `non-equilibrium-statistical-mechanics/driven-systems-and-nonequilibrium-steady-states` — Steady states maintained by currents — exclusion processes, KPZ interface growth, absorbing-state transitions and self-organized criticality. · $\partial_th=\nu\nabla^2h+\frac\lambda2(\nabla h)^2+\eta,\qquad J_{\text{TASEP}}=\rho(1-\rho)$

  - **Linear response and fluctuation–dissipation** `non-equilibrium-statistical-mechanics/linear-response-and-fluctuation-dissipation` — How systems respond to weak perturbations — response functions, Kubo formulas, the fluctuation–dissipation theorem and Onsager reciprocity. · $S_x(\omega)=\frac{2k_BT}{\omega}\,\mathrm{Im}\,\chi(\omega),\qquad \sigma=\frac{1}{3Vk_BT}\int_0^\infty\langle\mathbf J(t)\cdot\mathbf J(0)\rangle dt$

  - **Critical exponents and universality** `phase-transitions-and-critical-phenomena/critical-exponents-and-universality` — Power-law singularities at critical points, scaling relations, universality classes and their precise numerical values. · $\xi\sim|t|^{-\nu},\quad C\sim|t|^{-\alpha},\quad M\sim(-t)^\beta,\quad \chi\sim|t|^{-\gamma},\quad G(r)\sim r^{-(d-2+\eta)}$
  - **Ising model and lattice models** `phase-transitions-and-critical-phenomena/ising-model-and-lattice-models` — The Ising, Potts, XY and Heisenberg models — the canonical systems for studying order, disorder and phase transitions. · $H=-J\sum_{\langle ij\rangle}s_is_j-h\sum_is_i,\qquad \beta_{2D}=\tfrac18,\ \nu_{2D}=1,\ \gamma_{2D}=\tfrac74$
  - **Mean-field and Landau theory** `phase-transitions-and-critical-phenomena/mean-field-and-landau-theory` — Approximating interacting systems by an average field; Landau free-energy expansions in the order parameter; Ginzburg criterion. · $F=a(T-T_c)\phi^2+b\phi^4\Rightarrow\phi\propto(T_c-T)^{1/2};\qquad \beta=\tfrac12,\ \gamma=1,\ \delta=3,\ \alpha=0\ (\text{MF})$
  - **Renormalization group** `phase-transitions-and-critical-phenomena/renormalization-group` — Coarse-graining as a flow in the space of theories — block spins, fixed points, relevant/irrelevant operators and the epsilon expansion. · $\nu=\frac1{y_t},\qquad \nu=\frac12+\frac{\epsilon}{12}+\mathcal O(\epsilon^2)\ (\text{Ising},\ n=1)$
  - **Topological phase transitions (BKT)** `phase-transitions-and-critical-phenomena/topological-phase-transitions-bkt` — The Berezinskii–Kosterlitz–Thouless transition — vortex–antivortex unbinding, quasi-long-range order and the universal superfluid jump in 2D. · $F_{\text{vortex}}=(\pi J-2k_BT)\ln\frac La\Rightarrow k_BT_{\text{BKT}}\approx\frac{\pi J}{2}\ (\text{bare})$

  - **CP violation** `standard-model/cp-violation` — Violation of combined charge-conjugation and parity symmetry in kaons, B mesons and possibly neutrinos; link to the baryon asymmetry. · $J=\mathrm{Im}(V_{us}V_{cb}V_{ub}^*V_{cs}^*),\qquad \eta_B=\frac{n_B-n_{\bar B}}{n_\gamma}\approx6\times10^{-10}$
  - **Electroweak theory** `standard-model/electroweak-theory` — The unified SU(2)×U(1) theory of weak and electromagnetic interactions, symmetry breaking, and precision electroweak tests. · $\rho=\frac{m_W^2}{m_Z^2\cos^2\theta_W}=1\ (\text{tree level}),\qquad \sin^2\theta_W^{\text{eff}}\approx0.2315$
  - **Flavor physics and the CKM matrix** `standard-model/flavor-physics-and-ckm` — Quark mixing, the CKM matrix and unitarity triangle, rare decays and flavor anomalies as probes of new physics.
  - **Hadron spectroscopy and exotic hadrons** `standard-model/hadron-spectroscopy-and-exotics` — The quark model of mesons and baryons, and the tetraquarks, pentaquarks and hybrids discovered since 2003. · $\text{Gell-Mann–Okubo: } 2(m_N+m_\Xi)=3m_\Lambda+m_\Sigma$
  - **Higgs boson** `standard-model/higgs-boson` — The scalar boson of electroweak symmetry breaking — production, decays, couplings and self-interaction. · $g_{hff}=\frac{m_f}{v},\qquad g_{hVV}=\frac{2m_V^2}{v},\qquad \lambda_{hhh}^{\text{SM}}=\frac{m_h^2}{2v^2}\approx0.13$
  - **Quantum chromodynamics** `standard-model/quantum-chromodynamics` — The SU(3) gauge theory of quarks and gluons — asymptotic freedom, confinement, chiral symmetry breaking and hadron structure. · $\mathcal L_{\text{QCD}}=\sum_q\bar q\,(i\gamma^\mu D_\mu-m_q)\,q-\tfrac14G^a_{\mu\nu}G^{a\,\mu\nu},\qquad m_\pi^2f_\pi^2=-(m_u+m_d)\langle\bar qq\rangle$

### 07 Nuclear Physics · `nuclear-physicist`
The atomic nucleus and strongly interacting matter — nuclear structure, reactions and decays, nucleon structure, heavy-ion collisions, nuclear astrophysics and applications.

- **Applied nuclear physics** `applied-nuclear-physics` — Nuclear physics put to work — reactors and nuclear energy, radiation detection and dosimetry, nuclear data and isotope production. · $k_{\text{eff}}=\eta f p\varepsilon P_{\text{FNL}}P_{\text{TNL}},\qquad I(x)=I_0e^{-\mu x},\qquad 1\ \text{Gy}=1\ \text{J/kg},\quad H=w_RD$
  - **Nuclear data and isotope production** `applied-nuclear-physics/nuclear-data-and-isotope-production` — Evaluated nuclear data libraries and mass tables, and production of isotopes for medicine, industry and research. · $A(t)=N_{\text{target}}\,\sigma\,\phi\,(1-e^{-\lambda t})\quad(\text{activation during irradiation})$
  - **Nuclear reactors and energy** `applied-nuclear-physics/nuclear-reactors-and-energy` — Fission reactors — chain reactions, the four/six-factor formula, moderation, reactor kinetics, reactor types, safety, fuel cycles and waste. · $P(t)\approx P_0e^{t/T},\ T\approx\frac{\beta-\rho}{\lambda\rho}\ (\rho<\beta),\qquad \rho=\frac{k-1}{k}$
  - **Radiation detection and dosimetry** `applied-nuclear-physics/radiation-detection-and-dosimetry` — How ionizing radiation interacts with matter and how it is detected and quantified — gas, scintillation and semiconductor detectors; absorbed dose and radiation protection. · $\frac{\sigma_E}{E}=2.355\sqrt{\frac{F\,w}{E}},\qquad \sigma_{\text{PE}}\propto\frac{Z^{4\text{–}5}}{E^{3.5}}$
- **Hadron and nucleon structure** `hadron-and-nucleon-structure` — The internal quark–gluon structure of protons and neutrons — deep inelastic scattering, parton distributions, spin, form factors and the Electron–Ion Collider. · $F_2(x,Q^2)=\sum_qe_q^2\,x\,[q(x)+\bar q(x)],\qquad \frac{\partial q(x,\mu^2)}{\partial\ln\mu^2}=\frac{\alpha_s}{2\pi}\int_x^1\frac{dz}{z}P_{qq}(z)\,q\!\left(\frac xz\right)+\cdots$
  - **Electron–Ion Collider** `hadron-and-nucleon-structure/electron-ion-collider` — The next-generation QCD facility at Brookhaven — gluon saturation, 3D imaging of nucleons and nuclei, and the origin of nucleon mass and spin. · $Q_s^2(x,A)\sim A^{1/3}\left(\frac{x_0}{x}\right)^{\lambda},\ \lambda\approx0.3$
  - **Nucleon spin and form factors** `hadron-and-nucleon-structure/nucleon-spin-and-form-factors` — The proton spin puzzle, electromagnetic form factors, the proton charge radius, and generalized and transverse-momentum-dependent distributions. · $\langle r_E^2\rangle=-6\frac{dG_E}{dQ^2}\Big|_{Q^2=0},\qquad J_q=\tfrac12\int x\,[H_q(x,\xi,0)+E_q(x,\xi,0)]\,dx$
  - **Parton distribution functions** `hadron-and-nucleon-structure/parton-distribution-functions` — The momentum distributions of quarks and gluons inside hadrons — deep inelastic scattering, Bjorken scaling, DGLAP evolution and global PDF fits. · $\int_0^1dx\,x\sum_i f_i(x,\mu^2)=1,\qquad \int_0^1[u(x)-\bar u(x)]dx=2$
- **Heavy-ion physics** `heavy-ion-physics` — Recreating the early universe's quark–gluon plasma in collisions of heavy nuclei — collective flow, the QCD phase diagram and jet quenching. · $\frac{dN}{d\phi}\propto1+2\sum_nv_n\cos[n(\phi-\Psi_n)],\qquad \epsilon_{\text{Bj}}=\frac{1}{\pi R^2\tau_0}\frac{dE_T}{dy},\qquad T_c\approx156\ \text{MeV}$
  - **Collective flow and hydrodynamics** `heavy-ion-physics/collective-flow-and-hydrodynamics` — Anisotropic flow in heavy-ion collisions, relativistic viscous hydrodynamics, initial-state fluctuations and flow in small systems. · $\partial_\mu T^{\mu\nu}=0,\quad T^{\mu\nu}=(\varepsilon+P)u^\mu u^\nu-Pg^{\mu\nu}+\pi^{\mu\nu},\qquad v_n\approx\kappa_n\varepsilon_n$
  - **Jet quenching** `heavy-ion-physics/jet-quenching` — Energy loss of high-energy quarks and gluons traversing the quark–gluon plasma — nuclear modification factors, jet substructure modification, transport coefficient q-hat. · $R_{AA}=\frac{dN_{AA}/dp_T}{\langle N_{\text{coll}}\rangle\,dN_{pp}/dp_T},\qquad \Delta E_{\text{rad}}\propto\alpha_s\hat qL^2$
  - **QCD phase diagram and critical point** `heavy-ion-physics/qcd-phase-diagram-and-critical-point` — Phases of strongly interacting matter versus temperature and baryon density — crossover, possible critical point, color superconductivity and beam-energy scans. · $\kappa\sigma^2=\frac{C_4}{C_2},\qquad \chi_n^B=\frac{\partial^n(P/T^4)}{\partial(\mu_B/T)^n}$
  - **Quark–gluon plasma** `heavy-ion-physics/quark-gluon-plasma` — Deconfined quarks and gluons at high temperature — signatures, thermodynamics, transport properties and the "perfect liquid". · $\frac{\varepsilon}{T^4}\approx\frac{\pi^2}{30}\left(16+\frac{21}{2}n_f\right)\ (\text{ideal QGP}),\qquad \frac\eta s\gtrsim\frac{\hbar}{4\pi k_B}$
- **Nuclear astrophysics** `nuclear-astrophysics` — Nuclear physics of the cosmos — Big Bang nucleosynthesis, stellar burning and element synthesis, the r-process, and dense matter in neutron stars. · $4p\to{}^4\text{He}+2e^++2\nu_e+26.7\ \text{MeV},\qquad Y_p\approx\frac{2(n/p)}{1+(n/p)}\approx0.25,\qquad \frac{dP}{dr}=-\frac{G(\varepsilon+P)(m+4\pi r^3P/c^2)}{c^2r(r-2Gm/c^2)}$
  - **Big Bang nucleosynthesis** `nuclear-astrophysics/big-bang-nucleosynthesis` — Formation of D, ³He, ⁴He and ⁷Li in the first minutes — neutron–proton freeze-out, the deuterium bottleneck, abundances and the lithium problem. · $\frac{n}{p}=e^{-Q/k_BT_f},\ Q=1.293\ \text{MeV};\qquad \eta_{10}=273.9\,\Omega_bh^2$
  - **Neutron-star equation of state** `nuclear-astrophysics/neutron-star-equation-of-state` — Matter above nuclear saturation density — the equation of state, maximum mass, radii from NICER, tidal deformability from gravitational waves, exotic phases. · $\Lambda=\frac23k_2\left(\frac{c^2R}{GM}\right)^5,\qquad E(n,\delta)\approx E_0(n)+S(n)\delta^2,\ \delta=\frac{n_n-n_p}{n}$
  - **Stellar nucleosynthesis and the r-process** `nuclear-astrophysics/stellar-nucleosynthesis-and-the-r-process` — How stars build elements — hydrogen and helium burning, advanced burning stages, the s-process and the rapid neutron-capture (r-) process in mergers. · $3\,{}^4\text{He}\to{}^{12}\text{C}+7.27\ \text{MeV}\ (\text{via the }7.65\ \text{MeV Hoyle state}),\qquad \tau_n\ll\tau_\beta\ (\text{r}),\ \tau_n\gg\tau_\beta\ (\text{s})$
- **Nuclear reactions and decays** `nuclear-reactions-and-decays` — How nuclei transform — alpha, beta and gamma decay, reaction mechanisms and cross sections, fission and fusion. · $N(t)=N_0e^{-\lambda t},\qquad Q=\left(\sum m_i-\sum m_f\right)c^2,\qquad \sigma(E)=\frac{S(E)}{E}e^{-2\pi\eta},\ \eta=\frac{Z_1Z_2e^2}{4\pi\epsilon_0\hbar v}$
  - **Beta decay and weak interactions** `nuclear-reactions-and-decays/beta-decay-and-weak-interactions` — Fermi's theory of beta decay, allowed and forbidden transitions, superallowed decays and Vud, neutron lifetime, and tests of the weak interaction in nuclei. · $ft=\frac{K}{G_F^2|V_{ud}|^2(1+\Delta_R)\,2},\qquad \frac{d\Gamma}{dE_e}\propto F(Z,E_e)\,p_eE_e(E_0-E_e)^2$
  - **Fusion reactions** `nuclear-reactions-and-decays/fusion-reactions` — Light-nucleus fusion — Coulomb barrier tunnelling, the Gamow peak, astrophysical S-factors, D–T and other fuel cycles, and reaction-rate measurements. · $\mathrm{D}+\mathrm{T}\to{}^4\mathrm{He}\,(3.5\ \text{MeV})+n\,(14.1\ \text{MeV}),\qquad E_0=\left[\left(\frac{\pi\alpha Z_1Z_2k_BT}{\sqrt2}\right)^2\mu c^2\right]^{1/3}$
  - **Nuclear fission** `nuclear-reactions-and-decays/nuclear-fission` — The splitting of heavy nuclei — liquid-drop picture, fission barriers, fragment distributions, prompt neutrons and chain reactions. · $x=\frac{E_C^{(0)}}{2E_S^{(0)}}\approx\frac{Z^2/A}{50},\qquad E_{\text{fission}}\approx200\ \text{MeV}$
  - **Nuclear reactions and scattering** `nuclear-reactions-and-decays/nuclear-reactions-and-scattering` — Reaction mechanisms and their theory — Q-values, compound-nucleus and direct reactions, resonances, optical model and cross-section evaluation. · $\sigma_{\text{BW}}(E)=\frac{\pi}{k^2}g\frac{\Gamma_a\Gamma_b}{(E-E_R)^2+\Gamma^2/4},\qquad Q=T_f-T_i$
  - **Radioactive decay and alpha decay** `nuclear-reactions-and-decays/radioactive-decay-and-alpha-decay` — Exponential decay laws, decay chains, alpha decay by quantum tunnelling, the Geiger–Nuttall law, and gamma transitions. · $t_{1/2}=\frac{\ln2}{\lambda},\qquad \log_{10}t_{1/2}=a\frac{Z}{\sqrt{Q_\alpha}}+b\ (\text{Geiger–Nuttall}),\qquad P\approx e^{-2\int_R^{r_c}\sqrt{2\mu(V-E)}\,dr/\hbar}$
- **Nuclear structure** `nuclear-structure` — How nuclei are organized — binding energies, shell and collective models, nuclear forces from chiral EFT, ab initio many-body theory, exotic and superheavy nuclei. · $B(A,Z)=a_VA-a_SA^{2/3}-a_C\frac{Z(Z-1)}{A^{1/3}}-a_A\frac{(A-2Z)^2}{A}+\delta(A,Z),\qquad V(r)\sim-g^2\frac{e^{-m_\pi r}}{r}$
  - **Ab initio nuclear theory** `nuclear-structure/ab-initio-nuclear-theory` — Solving the nuclear many-body problem from realistic forces — no-core shell model, coupled cluster, IMSRG, quantum Monte Carlo and lattice EFT. · $H=\sum_i\frac{p_i^2}{2m}+\sum_{i<j}V_{ij}+\sum_{i<j<k}V_{ijk},\qquad \frac{dH_s}{ds}=[\eta_s,H_s]\ (\text{SRG/IMSRG})$
  - **Exotic nuclei and drip lines** `nuclear-structure/exotic-nuclei-and-drip-lines` — Nuclei far from stability — halo nuclei, shell evolution and islands of inversion, the proton and neutron drip lines, radioactive-beam facilities. · $S_n=B(A,Z)-B(A-1,Z),\qquad \text{drip line: }S_n\to0$
  - **Nuclear forces and chiral EFT** `nuclear-structure/nuclear-forces-and-chiral-eft` — The nucleon–nucleon interaction from meson exchange to chiral effective field theory, three-nucleon forces and lattice QCD constraints. · $V_{1\pi}(\mathbf q)=-\frac{g_A^2}{4f_\pi^2}\boldsymbol\tau_1\cdot\boldsymbol\tau_2\frac{(\boldsymbol\sigma_1\cdot\mathbf q)(\boldsymbol\sigma_2\cdot\mathbf q)}{q^2+m_\pi^2}$
  - **Shell model and collective models** `nuclear-structure/shell-model-and-collective-models` — Independent-particle shells with spin–orbit coupling, magic numbers, configuration interaction, and collective rotations, vibrations and deformation. · $V(r)=-\frac{V_0}{1+e^{(r-R)/a}}+V_{ls}(r)\,\mathbf l\cdot\mathbf s,\qquad \frac{E(4^+)}{E(2^+)}=\frac{10}{3}\ (\text{rigid rotor})$
  - **Superheavy elements** `nuclear-structure/superheavy-elements` — Synthesis and properties of elements beyond Z = 104, shell stabilization and the predicted island of stability. · $Z^2/A\gtrsim50\ \Rightarrow\ \text{no liquid-drop fission barrier}$

### 08 Condensed Matter Physics · `condensed-matter-physicist`
The physics of solids, liquids and quantum materials — electronic structure, superconductivity, magnetism, strong correlations, topological matter, 2D materials and soft matter.

- **Crystals and electronic structure** `crystals-and-electronic-structure` — Periodic solids and their electrons — crystal lattices and diffraction, band theory, phonons, semiconductors, density functional theory, and electronic transport. · $\psi_{n\mathbf k}(\mathbf r+\mathbf R)=e^{i\mathbf k\cdot\mathbf R}\psi_{n\mathbf k}(\mathbf r),\qquad 2d\sin\theta=n\lambda,\qquad \sigma=\frac{ne^2\tau}{m^*}$
  - **Band theory and Bloch electrons** `crystals-and-electronic-structure/band-theory-and-bloch-electrons` — Electrons in periodic potentials — Bloch's theorem, nearly-free-electron and tight-binding models, band gaps, effective mass, Fermi surfaces. · $E(\mathbf k)=\epsilon_0-2t(\cos k_xa+\cos k_ya)\ (\text{2D tight binding}),\qquad \Delta\!\left(\frac1B\right)=\frac{2\pi e}{\hbar A_F}$
  - **Crystal structure and diffraction** `crystals-and-electronic-structure/crystal-structure-and-diffraction` — Bravais lattices, unit cells, reciprocal lattices and Brillouin zones; X-ray, electron and neutron diffraction; quasicrystals. · $S_{\mathbf G}=\sum_jf_je^{-i\mathbf G\cdot\mathbf r_j},\qquad \mathbf b_1=2\pi\frac{\mathbf a_2\times\mathbf a_3}{\mathbf a_1\cdot(\mathbf a_2\times\mathbf a_3)}$
  - **Density functional theory** `crystals-and-electronic-structure/density-functional-theory` — First-principles electronic structure from the electron density — Hohenberg–Kohn theorems, Kohn–Sham equations, exchange–correlation functionals and their limits. · $\left[-\frac{\hbar^2}{2m}\nabla^2+v_{\text{ext}}+v_H[n]+v_{xc}[n]\right]\phi_i=\varepsilon_i\phi_i,\qquad n(\mathbf r)=\sum_i^{\text{occ}}|\phi_i|^2$
  - **Electronic transport and mesoscopic physics** `crystals-and-electronic-structure/electronic-transport-and-mesoscopic-physics` — How electrons carry current — Drude and Boltzmann transport, Hall effect, Landauer–Büttiker formalism, quantized conductance, weak localization and shot noise. · $G=\frac{2e^2}{h}\sum_nT_n,\qquad R_H=\frac{1}{nq},\qquad \frac{\kappa}{\sigma T}=\frac{\pi^2}{3}\left(\frac{k_B}{e}\right)^2$
  - **Phonons and lattice dynamics** `crystals-and-electronic-structure/phonons-and-lattice-dynamics` — Quantized lattice vibrations — dispersion relations, acoustic and optical branches, heat capacity, thermal transport, anharmonicity and electron–phonon coupling. · $C_V=\frac{12\pi^4}{5}Nk_B\left(\frac{T}{\Theta_D}\right)^3\ (T\ll\Theta_D),\qquad \kappa=\frac13C_Vv\ell$
  - **Semiconductors** `crystals-and-electronic-structure/semiconductors` — Intrinsic and doped semiconductors, carrier statistics, recombination, p–n junctions, heterostructures and the physics behind transistors and LEDs. · $np=n_i^2,\qquad I=I_0\left(e^{qV/k_BT}-1\right),\qquad D=\mu\frac{k_BT}{q}$
- **Low-dimensional and quantum materials** `low-dimensional-and-quantum-materials` — Atomically thin and nanoscale materials — graphene and 2D crystals, moiré superlattices and twisted bilayers, transition-metal dichalcogenides and excitons, quantum dots. · $E(\mathbf k)=\pm\hbar v_F|\mathbf k|,\ v_F\approx10^6\ \text{m/s};\qquad \lambda_{\text{moiré}}\approx\frac{a}{2\sin(\theta/2)}\approx\frac a\theta$
  - **Graphene and 2D materials** `low-dimensional-and-quantum-materials/graphene-and-2d-materials` — Atomically thin crystals — graphene's Dirac electrons, hexagonal boron nitride, van der Waals heterostructures and the wider family of 2D materials. · $H=\hbar v_F(\tau_z\sigma_xk_x+\sigma_yk_y),\qquad E_n=\pm v_F\sqrt{2e\hbar B|n|}$
  - **Moiré materials and twisted bilayers** `low-dimensional-and-quantum-materials/moire-materials-and-twisted-bilayers` — Superlattices from twisted or lattice-mismatched layers — magic-angle graphene, flat bands, correlated insulators, superconductivity and fractional Chern insulators. · $\theta_{\text{magic}}\approx\frac{3\sqrt3\,w}{4\pi\hbar v_F/a}\approx1.1^\circ\ (w\approx110\ \text{meV}),\qquad n_s=\frac{8\theta^2}{\sqrt3a^2}\ (\text{full filling})$
  - **Quantum dots and nanostructures** `low-dimensional-and-quantum-materials/quantum-dots-and-nanostructures` — Electrons confined in zero and one dimension — gate-defined and self-assembled quantum dots, colloidal nanocrystals, nanowires, Coulomb blockade and size-tunable optics. · $E_g(R)\approx E_g^{\text{bulk}}+\frac{\hbar^2\pi^2}{2R^2}\left(\frac{1}{m_e^*}+\frac{1}{m_h^*}\right)-\frac{1.8e^2}{4\pi\epsilon R},\qquad E_C=\frac{e^2}{C_\Sigma}\gg k_BT$
  - **Transition-metal dichalcogenides and excitons** `low-dimensional-and-quantum-materials/transition-metal-dichalcogenides-and-excitons` — Monolayer semiconductors like MoS₂ and WSe₂ — direct gaps, spin–valley locking, tightly bound excitons, interlayer excitons and exciton condensates. · $E_n^{2D}=-\frac{\mu e^4}{2(4\pi\epsilon\epsilon_0)^2\hbar^2(n-\tfrac12)^2}\ (\text{ideal 2D hydrogen}),\qquad \sigma^\pm\leftrightarrow K,K'$
- **Magnetism** `magnetism` — Magnetic order in solids — exchange interactions, ferro- and antiferromagnetism, spintronics, frustration and spin liquids, skyrmions and altermagnets. · $H=-\sum_{\langle ij\rangle}J_{ij}\,\mathbf S_i\cdot\mathbf S_j,\qquad \chi=\frac{C}{T-\theta_{CW}},\qquad \hbar\omega_{\mathbf k}\approx Dk^2\ (\text{ferromagnetic magnons})$
  - **Altermagnetism** `magnetism/altermagnetism` — A third class of collinear magnets — zero net magnetization like antiferromagnets, but spin-split bands like ferromagnets, set by crystal symmetry. · $E_\uparrow(\mathbf k)-E_\downarrow(\mathbf k)\propto k_xk_y\ (d\text{-wave}),\qquad \sum_{\mathbf k}\left[E_\uparrow-E_\downarrow\right]=0$
  - **Exchange and magnetic order** `magnetism/exchange-and-magnetic-order` — Direct, super- and RKKY exchange, Heisenberg and Ising models, ferro-, antiferro- and ferrimagnetism, domains, magnons and neutron scattering. · $J_{\text{super}}\approx\frac{4t^2}{U},\qquad M(T)=M(0)\left[1-a\,T^{3/2}\right],\qquad I\,N(E_F)>1\ (\text{Stoner})$
  - **Frustrated magnets and spin liquids** `magnetism/frustrated-magnets-and-spin-liquids` — Magnets whose interactions cannot all be satisfied — spin ice, kagome and triangular antiferromagnets, resonating valence bonds, Kitaev materials and quantum spin liquids. · $H_K=-\sum_{\langle ij\rangle_\gamma}K_\gamma S_i^\gamma S_j^\gamma,\qquad f=\frac{|\theta_{CW}|}{T_N}\gg1$
