  - **Spintronics** `magnetism/spintronics` — Using electron spin in devices — giant and tunnelling magnetoresistance, spin-transfer and spin–orbit torques, spin Hall effects and magnonics. · $\text{TMR}=\frac{2P_1P_2}{1-P_1P_2}\ (\text{Jullière}),\qquad \mathbf J_s=\theta_{SH}\frac{\hbar}{2e}\,\hat{\boldsymbol\sigma}\times\mathbf J_c$
- **Soft matter** `soft-matter` — Materials easily deformed by thermal fluctuations — polymers, liquid crystals, colloids and granular media, active matter, glasses and jamming.
  - **Active matter** `soft-matter/active-matter` — Systems of self-propelled units that consume energy — flocking, motility-induced phase separation, active nematics, living and synthetic active materials. · $\dot{\mathbf r}_i=v_0\hat{\mathbf e}(\theta_i)+\sqrt{2D_t}\boldsymbol\xi_i,\qquad \theta_i(t+1)=\langle\theta_j\rangle_{|r_{ij}|<R}+\eta_i\ (\text{Vicsek})$
  - **Colloids and granular matter** `soft-matter/colloids-and-granular-matter` — Micron-scale particles in fluids and athermal grains — Brownian motion, DLVO and depletion forces, colloidal crystals, granular packing, flow and force chains. · $D=\frac{k_BT}{6\pi\eta a},\qquad \mu(I)=\mu_s+\frac{\mu_2-\mu_s}{1+I_0/I},\ I=\frac{\dot\gamma d}{\sqrt{P/\rho}}$
  - **Glasses and jamming** `soft-matter/glasses-and-jamming` — Amorphous solids — supercooled liquids, the glass transition, dynamical heterogeneity, aging, and the jamming transition of athermal packings. · $\tau=\tau_0\exp\!\left(\frac{DT_0}{T-T_0}\right)\ (\text{VFT}),\qquad z_c=2d$
  - **Liquid crystals** `soft-matter/liquid-crystals` — Fluids with orientational order — nematics, smectics and cholesterics, the Frank elastic theory, topological defects, active nematics and display technology. · $S=\left\langle\tfrac12(3\cos^2\theta-1)\right\rangle,\qquad E_c=\frac{\pi}{d}\sqrt{\frac{K_1}{\epsilon_0\Delta\epsilon}}\ (\text{Fréedericksz})$
  - **Polymers** `soft-matter/polymers` — Long-chain molecules — random walks and excluded volume, Flory theory, scaling, rubber elasticity, entanglement and reptation, polymer solutions and gels. · $\langle R^2\rangle=Nb^2\ (\text{ideal}),\qquad f=\frac{3k_BT}{Nb^2}R,\qquad \tau_{\text{rep}}\sim N^3$
- **Strongly correlated electrons** `strongly-correlated-electrons` — Electrons whose interactions dominate — Mott insulators and the Hubbard model, Kondo physics and heavy fermions, quantum criticality, topological order and the numerics to tackle them. · $H_{\text{Hub}}=-t\sum_{\langle ij\rangle\sigma}(c^\dagger_{i\sigma}c_{j\sigma}+\text{h.c.})+U\sum_in_{i\uparrow}n_{i\downarrow},\qquad T_K\sim De^{-1/(J\rho)}$
  - **Fractionalization and topological order** `strongly-correlated-electrons/fractionalization-and-topological-order` — Phases beyond symmetry breaking — topological ground-state degeneracy, anyons, long-range entanglement, and their use for fault-tolerant quantum computation. · $S_A=\alpha L-\gamma,\ \gamma=\ln\mathcal D;\qquad \psi_m=\prod_{i<j}(z_i-z_j)^me^{-\sum|z_i|^2/4\ell_B^2}$
  - **Heavy fermions and Kondo physics** `strongly-correlated-electrons/heavy-fermions-and-kondo-physics` — Magnetic impurities and lattices of local moments in metals — the Kondo effect, heavy-fermion metals with enormous effective masses, and Kondo breakdown. · $\rho(T)=\rho_0+aT^5-c_K\ln\frac{T}{D},\qquad \gamma=\frac{C}{T}\propto m^*$
  - **Hubbard model and Mott physics** `strongly-correlated-electrons/hubbard-model-and-mott-physics` — The Hubbard model, Mott metal–insulator transitions, superexchange antiferromagnetism and the doped Mott insulator.
  - **Numerical many-body methods** `strongly-correlated-electrons/numerical-many-body-methods` — Computational tools for correlated quantum matter — exact diagonalization, quantum Monte Carlo, DMRG and tensor networks, DMFT, and neural-network states. · $G_{\text{loc}}(i\omega_n)=\sum_{\mathbf k}\frac{1}{i\omega_n+\mu-\epsilon_{\mathbf k}-\Sigma(i\omega_n)}\quad(\text{DMFT self-consistency})$
  - **Quantum criticality and strange metals** `strongly-correlated-electrons/quantum-criticality-and-strange-metals` — Continuous phase transitions at zero temperature, quantum critical fans, non-Fermi liquids and the linear-in-temperature resistivity of strange metals. · $\xi\sim|g-g_c|^{-\nu},\quad \Delta\sim|g-g_c|^{z\nu},\qquad \frac{\hbar}{\tau}\approx\alpha k_BT,\ \alpha\sim1$
- **Superconductivity** `superconductivity` — Zero resistance and perfect diamagnetism — BCS theory, cuprate, iron-based and nickelate superconductors, hydrides under pressure, Josephson physics. · $\Delta(0)\approx1.764\,k_BT_c,\qquad \lambda_L=\sqrt{\frac{m}{\mu_0n_se^2}},\qquad \Phi_0=\frac{h}{2e}\approx2.07\times10^{-15}\ \text{Wb}$
  - **BCS theory and conventional superconductors** `superconductivity/bcs-theory-and-conventional-superconductors` — Cooper pairing, the BCS ground state and gap, the Meissner effect, London and Ginzburg–Landau theories, type-I and type-II superconductors. · $k_BT_c\approx1.13\,\hbar\omega_De^{-1/N(0)V},\qquad E_{\mathbf k}=\sqrt{\xi_{\mathbf k}^2+\Delta^2},\qquad \kappa=\frac{\lambda}{\xi}\gtrless\frac{1}{\sqrt2}$
  - **Cuprate high-Tc superconductors** `superconductivity/cuprate-high-tc-superconductors` — Copper-oxide superconductors — doped Mott insulators, d-wave pairing, the pseudogap, charge order and the strange-metal normal state. · $\Delta(\mathbf k)=\frac{\Delta_0}{2}(\cos k_xa-\cos k_ya),\qquad \rho(T)\approx\rho_0+AT\ (\text{strange metal})$
  - **Hydride superconductors under pressure** `superconductivity/hydride-superconductors-under-pressure` — Hydrogen-rich compounds superconducting near room temperature at megabar pressures — H₃S, LaH₁₀, predictions, measurement challenges and retracted claims. · $T_c=\frac{\omega_{\log}}{1.2}\exp\!\left[-\frac{1.04(1+\lambda)}{\lambda-\mu^*(1+0.62\lambda)}\right]\ (\text{Allen–Dynes})$
  - **Iron-based and nickelate superconductors** `superconductivity/iron-based-and-nickelate-superconductors` — Iron pnictides and chalcogenides with multi-band s± pairing, and nickelates — infinite-layer thin films and high-pressure Ruddlesden–Popper phases near 80 K. · $\Delta_h=-\Delta_e\ (s^\pm),\qquad \mathbf Q=(\pi,0)\ \text{nesting vector}$
  - **Josephson effect and SQUIDs** `superconductivity/josephson-effect-and-squids` — Cooper-pair tunnelling between superconductors — DC and AC Josephson effects, flux quantization, SQUID magnetometers and voltage standards. · $I=I_c\sin\varphi,\qquad \frac{d\varphi}{dt}=\frac{2eV}{\hbar},\qquad f=\frac{2e}{h}V\approx483.6\ \text{GHz/mV}$
- **Topological matter** `topological-matter` — Phases of matter classified by topology — quantum Hall effects, topological insulators and semimetals, topological superconductors and Majorana modes, band topology. · $\sigma_{xy}=\nu\frac{e^2}{h},\qquad C=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\text{BZ}}\Omega_{xy}(\mathbf k)\,d^2k,\qquad H_{\text{surf}}=\hbar v_F(\sigma_xk_y-\sigma_yk_x)$
  - **Band topology and Berry curvature** `topological-matter/band-topology-and-berry-curvature` — Geometric and topological properties of Bloch bands — Berry connection and curvature, Chern and Z₂ invariants, the tenfold way, and topological quantum chemistry.
  - **Quantum Hall effects** `topological-matter/quantum-hall-effects` — Quantized Hall conductance in 2D electron systems — integer and fractional quantum Hall effects, edge states, and the anomalous quantum Hall effect without magnetic field. · $E_n=\hbar\omega_c\left(n+\tfrac12\right),\quad \omega_c=\frac{eB}{m^*},\qquad R_{xy}=\frac{h}{\nu e^2},\qquad n_\phi=\frac{eB}{h}$
  - **Topological insulators** `topological-matter/topological-insulators` — Insulators with protected metallic boundaries — the quantum spin Hall effect, 3D topological insulators, Z₂ invariants and surface Dirac fermions. · $(-1)^\nu=\prod_{i=1}^{8}\prod_{m}\xi_{2m}(\Gamma_i)\ (\text{Fu–Kane parity formula}),\qquad \mathcal L_\theta=\frac{\theta e^2}{2\pi h}\mathbf E\cdot\mathbf B,\ \theta=\pi$
  - **Topological semimetals** `topological-matter/topological-semimetals` — Gapless topological phases — Weyl and Dirac semimetals, Fermi arcs, chiral anomaly in solids, and nodal-line semimetals. · $H_W=\pm\hbar v_F\,\boldsymbol\sigma\cdot\mathbf k,\qquad \frac{dN_\pm}{dt}=\pm\frac{e^2}{h^2}\mathbf E\cdot\mathbf B$
  - **Topological superconductors and Majoranas** `topological-matter/topological-superconductors-and-majoranas` — Superconductors with topological order in their quasiparticle bands — the Kitaev chain, Majorana zero modes in nanowires and vortices, braiding and topological qubits. · $H_K=\sum_j\left[-\mu c_j^\dagger c_j-t(c_j^\dagger c_{j+1}+\text{h.c.})+\Delta(c_jc_{j+1}+\text{h.c.})\right],\quad |\mu|<2t\ \Rightarrow\ \text{topological}$

### 09 Astrophysics and Cosmology · `astrophysicist`
The physics of stars, galaxies and the universe as a whole — stellar astrophysics, galaxies, cosmology, dark matter, high-energy and astroparticle physics, planets and exoplanets.

- **Cosmology** `cosmology` — The universe as a physical system — FLRW expansion, the CMB, inflation, dark energy, large-scale structure and BAO, the Hubble tension and gravitational lensing.
  - **Cosmic microwave background** `cosmology/cosmic-microwave-background` — The relic radiation from recombination — its blackbody spectrum, temperature and polarization anisotropies, acoustic peaks, lensing and the parameters they determine. · $\frac{\Delta T}{T}(\hat n)=\sum_{\ell m}a_{\ell m}Y_{\ell m}(\hat n),\qquad C_\ell=\langle|a_{\ell m}|^2\rangle,\qquad T_0=2.7255\ \text{K}$
  - **Dark energy** `cosmology/dark-energy` — The cause of cosmic acceleration — the cosmological constant and its problem, dynamical dark energy, supernova and BAO measurements, and DESI's hints of evolution. · $w(a)=w_0+w_a(1-a),\qquad \rho_\Lambda\approx(2.24\ \text{meV})^4,\qquad q_0=\tfrac12\Omega_m-\Omega_\Lambda\approx-0.53$
  - **FLRW cosmology and expansion history** `cosmology/flrw-cosmology-and-expansion-history` — Homogeneous, isotropic cosmological models — the FLRW metric, Friedmann equations, cosmic components, distances and the ΛCDM parameters. · $ds^2=-c^2dt^2+a^2(t)\left[\frac{dr^2}{1-kr^2}+r^2d\Omega^2\right],\qquad d_L=(1+z)\,c\int_0^z\frac{dz'}{H(z')}$
  - **Gravitational lensing** `cosmology/gravitational-lensing` — Bending of light by mass — strong lensing and Einstein rings, weak lensing and cosmic shear, microlensing, time-delay cosmography.
  - **Hubble tension and cosmological tensions** `cosmology/hubble-tension-and-cosmological-tensions` — The ~5σ disagreement between local and early-universe determinations of H₀, the S₈ tension, and proposed systematic and new-physics explanations. · $H_0^{\text{SH0ES}}=73.0\pm1.0,\qquad H_0^{\text{Planck}}=67.4\pm0.5\ \text{km s}^{-1}\text{Mpc}^{-1},\qquad S_8=\sigma_8\sqrt{\Omega_m/0.3}$
  - **Inflation** `cosmology/inflation` — A period of accelerated expansion in the very early universe — solving the horizon and flatness problems, generating primordial fluctuations, and its observational tests. · $\epsilon=\frac{M_P^2}{2}\left(\frac{V'}{V}\right)^2,\quad \eta=M_P^2\frac{V''}{V},\qquad n_s-1\approx2\eta-6\epsilon,\quad r\approx16\epsilon$
  - **Large-scale structure and BAO** `cosmology/large-scale-structure-and-bao` — The cosmic web — growth of density perturbations, the matter power spectrum, galaxy surveys, redshift-space distortions and baryon acoustic oscillations. · $\ddot\delta+2H\dot\delta-4\pi G\bar\rho\delta=0,\qquad r_d=\int_{z_d}^\infty\frac{c_s(z)}{H(z)}dz\approx147\ \text{Mpc}$
- **Dark matter** `dark-matter` — Non-luminous matter making up ~85% of all matter — astrophysical evidence, direct and indirect detection searches, modified-gravity alternatives and small-scale structure. · $\Omega_ch^2\approx0.120,\qquad \rho_{\text{NFW}}(r)=\frac{\rho_s}{(r/r_s)(1+r/r_s)^2},\qquad \rho_\odot^{\text{DM}}\approx0.3\text{–}0.4\ \text{GeV cm}^{-3}$
  - **Alternatives: MOND and modified gravity** `dark-matter/alternatives-mond-and-modified-gravity` — Modified Newtonian dynamics and relativistic extensions as alternatives to particle dark matter — successes on galaxy scales, problems on cluster and cosmological scales. · $\mu\!\left(\frac{|\mathbf a|}{a_0}\right)\mathbf a=\mathbf a_N,\qquad v_\infty^4=GM_ba_0$
  - **Astrophysical evidence for dark matter** `dark-matter/astrophysical-evidence-for-dark-matter` — The multiple, independent lines of evidence — galaxy rotation curves, cluster dynamics and lensing, the Bullet Cluster, CMB peaks and structure formation. · $v_c^2=\frac{GM(<r)}{r}=\text{const}\Rightarrow\rho\propto r^{-2},\qquad \frac{\Omega_c}{\Omega_b}\approx5.4$
  - **Dark-matter small-scale structure** `dark-matter/dark-matter-small-scale-structure` — Testing dark-matter models on sub-galactic scales — cusp–core, missing satellites, too-big-to-fail, and self-interacting, warm and fuzzy dark matter. · $\lambda_{\text{dB}}=\frac{h}{mv}\approx1.2\ \text{kpc}\left(\frac{10^{-22}\,\text{eV}}{m}\right)\left(\frac{100\ \text{km/s}}{v}\right)$
  - **Direct detection** `dark-matter/direct-detection` — Searching for dark-matter particles scattering in terrestrial detectors — liquid-noble TPCs, cryogenic bolometers, low-mass techniques and the neutrino fog. · $\frac{dR}{dE_R}=\frac{\rho_\chi}{m_\chi m_N}\int_{v_{\min}}^{\infty}vf(v)\frac{d\sigma}{dE_R}d^3v,\qquad v_{\min}=\sqrt{\frac{m_NE_R}{2\mu^2}}$
  - **Indirect detection** `dark-matter/indirect-detection` — Looking for dark-matter annihilation or decay products — gamma rays, cosmic-ray antimatter, neutrinos and X-ray lines from halos and dwarf galaxies. · $\frac{d\Phi}{dE}=\frac{\langle\sigma v\rangle}{8\pi m_\chi^2}\frac{dN}{dE}\,J,\qquad J=\int_{\Delta\Omega}\int_{\text{l.o.s.}}\rho^2\,dl\,d\Omega$
- **Galaxies** `galaxies` — How galaxies form and evolve — hierarchical structure formation, the Milky Way, active galactic nuclei and supermassive black holes, galaxy clusters and the high-redshift universe. · $M_J\sim\frac{\pi^{5/2}}{6}\frac{c_s^3}{G^{3/2}\rho^{1/2}},\qquad v_c(r)=\sqrt{\frac{GM(<r)}{r}},\qquad M_{\text{BH}}\propto\sigma^{4\text{–}5}$
  - **Active galactic nuclei and supermassive black holes** `galaxies/active-galactic-nuclei-and-supermassive-black-holes` — Accreting supermassive black holes — quasars, Seyferts and blazars, unified models, relativistic jets, black-hole–galaxy co-evolution and feedback. · $L=\eta\dot Mc^2,\qquad M_{\text{BH}}=f\frac{R_{\text{BLR}}\,\Delta v^2}{G},\qquad \beta_{\text{app}}=\frac{\beta\sin\theta}{1-\beta\cos\theta}$
  - **Galaxy clusters** `galaxies/galaxy-clusters` — The largest gravitationally bound structures — dark-matter-dominated mass budgets, hot intracluster gas, the Sunyaev–Zel'dovich effect and clusters as cosmological probes. · $M_{\text{vir}}\approx\frac{3\sigma_v^2R}{G},\qquad \frac{\Delta T}{T_{\text{CMB}}}=-2y,\ y=\frac{\sigma_T}{m_ec^2}\int n_ek_BT_e\,dl$
  - **Galaxy formation and evolution** `galaxies/galaxy-formation-and-evolution` — From dark-matter halos to galaxies — gas cooling, star formation, feedback, morphology, scaling relations and cosmological simulations. · $\Sigma_{\text{SFR}}\propto\Sigma_{\text{gas}}^{1.4},\qquad L\propto v_{\max}^4\ (\text{Tully–Fisher})$
  - **High-redshift universe and reionization** `galaxies/high-redshift-universe-and-reionization` — The first stars and galaxies, cosmic dawn, the epoch of reionization, the 21-cm signal and JWST discoveries at z > 10. · $\tau_{GP}\approx1.8\times10^5\,h^{-1}\Omega_m^{-1/2}\frac{\Omega_bh^2}{0.02}\left(\frac{1+z}{7}\right)^{3/2}x_{\text{HI}},\qquad \lambda_{21}=21.1\ \text{cm}\ (1+z)$
  - **The Milky Way** `galaxies/the-milky-way` — Our galaxy's structure, dynamics and history — disk, bulge, bar and halo, Gaia's 6D map, galactic archaeology, and the Galactic Centre black hole. · $v_\odot\approx233\ \text{km s}^{-1},\ R_\odot\approx8.2\ \text{kpc};\qquad M_{\text{Sgr A}^*}\approx4.3\times10^6\,M_\odot$
- **High-energy astrophysics and astroparticle physics** `high-energy-astrophysics-and-astroparticle` — The violent universe and its messengers — cosmic rays, gamma-ray bursts, high-energy neutrinos, multi-messenger events, fast radio bursts and magnetars. · $\frac{dN}{dE}\propto E^{-2}\ (\text{diffusive shock acceleration}),\qquad E_{\max}\sim ZeBRc\beta\ (\text{Hillas}),\qquad E_{\text{GZK}}\approx5\times10^{19}\ \text{eV}$
  - **Cosmic rays** `high-energy-astrophysics-and-astroparticle/cosmic-rays` — Relativistic charged particles from space — the energy spectrum and composition, Galactic and extragalactic sources, diffusive shock acceleration and the GZK cutoff. · $E_{\text{GZK}}\approx\frac{m_\pi(m_\pi+2m_p)c^4}{4E_{\gamma}}\approx6\times10^{19}\ \text{eV},\qquad r_L=\frac{E}{ZeBc}\approx1.1\ \text{kpc}\frac{E/\text{EeV}}{Z\,B/\mu\text{G}}$
  - **Fast radio bursts and magnetars** `high-energy-astrophysics-and-astroparticle/fast-radio-bursts-and-magnetars` — Millisecond radio flashes from cosmological distances, repeaters and non-repeaters, the magnetar connection, and FRBs as probes of intergalactic baryons.
  - **Gamma-ray bursts** `high-energy-astrophysics-and-astroparticle/gamma-ray-bursts` — The most luminous explosions — long GRBs from collapsing massive stars, short GRBs from compact mergers, relativistic jets, fireball physics and afterglows. · $t_{\text{obs}}\approx\frac{R}{2\Gamma^2c},\qquad \theta_j\approx\frac{1}{\Gamma(t_{\text{jet}})}$
  - **Multi-messenger astronomy** `high-energy-astrophysics-and-astroparticle/multi-messenger-astronomy` — Combining gravitational waves, photons, neutrinos and cosmic rays from the same sources — GW170817, kilonovae, alert networks and what they teach. · $\frac{|\Delta v|}{c}\lesssim\frac{c\,\Delta t}{D}=\frac{1.7\ \text{s}\times c}{40\ \text{Mpc}}\approx4\times10^{-16}$
  - **Neutrino astronomy** `high-energy-astrophysics-and-astroparticle/neutrino-astronomy` — Observing the universe with neutrinos — IceCube and KM3NeT, the diffuse astrophysical flux, blazar and Seyfert sources, the Galactic plane and SN 1987A. · $p+\gamma\to\Delta^+\to n+\pi^+,\qquad E_\nu\approx\frac{E_p}{20}$
- **Planetary science and exoplanets** `planetary-science-and-exoplanets` — Planets in our Solar System and around other stars — detection methods, atmospheres, formation in protoplanetary disks, orbital dynamics and habitability. · $K=\left(\frac{2\pi G}{P}\right)^{1/3}\frac{M_p\sin i}{(M_\star+M_p)^{2/3}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-e^2}},\qquad \frac{\Delta F}{F}\approx\left(\frac{R_p}{R_\star}\right)^2$
  - **Astrobiology and habitability** `planetary-science-and-exoplanets/astrobiology-and-habitability` — Conditions for life beyond Earth — habitable zones, planetary climate and atmospheric retention, biosignature gases, Solar System ocean worlds and the search for life. · $d_{\text{HZ}}\approx\sqrt{\frac{L_\star}{L_\odot}}\ \text{AU}\times(0.95\text{–}1.67),\qquad T_{\text{eq}}=T_\star\sqrt{\frac{R_\star}{2a}}(1-A)^{1/4}$
  - **Exoplanet atmospheres** `planetary-science-and-exoplanets/exoplanet-atmospheres` — Measuring the composition and structure of exoplanet atmospheres — transmission and emission spectroscopy, phase curves, clouds and hazes, JWST results. · $H=\frac{k_BT}{\mu m_Hg},\qquad \delta(\lambda)\approx\frac{R_p^2+2R_pN_HH}{R_\star^2}$
  - **Exoplanet detection** `planetary-science-and-exoplanets/exoplanet-detection` — Finding planets around other stars — radial velocity, transits, microlensing, direct imaging, astrometry and timing; survey demographics. · $K_\oplus\approx0.09\ \text{m/s},\qquad K_{\text{Jup}}\approx12.5\ \text{m/s},\qquad P_{\text{transit}}\approx\frac{R_\star}{a}$
  - **Planet formation and protoplanetary disks** `planetary-science-and-exoplanets/planet-formation-and-protoplanetary-disks` — How planets form — protoplanetary disk physics, dust growth, pebble accretion, core accretion vs. gravitational instability, migration, and ALMA disk images. · $\nu=\alpha c_sH,\qquad \text{St}=\Omega_K t_s,\qquad M_{\text{iso}}\approx\frac{(2\pi\cdot2\sqrt3\,\Sigma_pa^2)^{3/2}}{(3M_\star)^{1/2}}$
  - **Solar System dynamics** `planetary-science-and-exoplanets/solar-system-dynamics` — Orbits and evolution of planets and small bodies — resonances, chaos, the Nice model of giant-planet migration, asteroids, Kuiper belt and comets. · $\frac{P_1}{P_2}\approx\frac{p}{q}\ (\text{mean-motion resonance}),\qquad \lambda_{\text{Lyap}}^{-1}\approx5\ \text{Myr}$
- **Stellar astrophysics** `stellar-astrophysics` — How stars are built, shine, evolve and die — stellar structure, the Sun, supernovae and transients, compact remnants, binaries and stellar populations.
  - **Binary stars and stellar populations** `stellar-astrophysics/binary-stars-and-stellar-populations` — Binaries and mass transfer, the initial mass function, star clusters, population synthesis and stellar archaeology. · $\frac{r_L}{a}\approx\frac{0.49q^{2/3}}{0.6q^{2/3}+\ln(1+q^{1/3})}\ (\text{Eggleton}),\qquad \xi(M)\propto M^{-2.35}$
  - **Compact objects** `stellar-astrophysics/compact-objects` — White dwarfs, neutron stars, pulsars, magnetars and stellar-mass black holes — degenerate matter, maximum masses and observational signatures. · $M_{\text{Ch}}\approx1.44\left(\frac{2}{\mu_e}\right)^2M_\odot,\qquad B\approx3.2\times10^{19}\sqrt{P\dot P}\ \text{G},\qquad \tau_c=\frac{P}{2\dot P}$
  - **Stellar structure and evolution** `stellar-astrophysics/stellar-structure-and-evolution` — The equations of stellar structure, energy transport, the HR diagram, and evolution from protostar through main sequence to giant phases and remnants. · $t_{KH}=\frac{GM^2}{RL}\approx3\times10^7\ \text{yr}\ (\text{Sun}),\qquad \left|\frac{d\ln T}{d\ln P}\right|_{\text{rad}}>\frac{\gamma-1}{\gamma}\Rightarrow\text{convection}$
  - **Supernovae and transients** `stellar-astrophysics/supernovae-and-transients` — Stellar explosions and other transients — core-collapse and Type Ia supernovae, light curves, remnants, and time-domain surveys like ZTF and Rubin. · $E_{\text{grav}}\approx\frac{3}{5}\frac{GM_{\text{NS}}^2}{R_{\text{NS}}}\approx3\times10^{46}\ \text{J},\qquad L(t)\propto e^{-t/\tau_{\text{Co}}}$
  - **The Sun and helioseismology** `stellar-astrophysics/the-sun-and-helioseismology` — The Sun as the best-studied star — standard solar models, helioseismic inversion of the interior, solar neutrinos, the solar abundance problem and the dynamo. · $\nu_{n\ell}\approx\Delta\nu\left(n+\frac\ell2+\epsilon\right),\qquad \Delta\nu\propto\sqrt{\bar\rho}$

### 10 Plasma Physics · `plasma-physicist`
Ionized matter dominated by collective electromagnetic behaviour — plasma fundamentals, magnetohydrodynamics, magnetic and inertial fusion, laser plasmas, space plasmas and applications.

- **Inertial and laser plasmas** `inertial-and-laser-plasmas` — Plasmas created and driven by intense lasers and particle beams — inertial confinement fusion, laser–plasma interactions, plasma wakefield acceleration and high-energy-density physics.
  - **High-energy-density physics** `inertial-and-laser-plasmas/high-energy-density-physics` — Matter at pressures above a megabar — warm dense matter, equations of state at extreme conditions, planetary interiors, X-ray Thomson scattering and laboratory astrophysics. · $P\gtrsim1\ \text{Mbar}=10^{11}\ \text{Pa},\qquad \theta=\frac{k_BT}{E_F}\sim1,\quad \Gamma\sim1$
  - **Inertial confinement fusion** `inertial-and-laser-plasmas/inertial-confinement-fusion` — Fusion by imploding fuel capsules — direct and indirect drive, hot-spot ignition, hydrodynamic instabilities, NIF burning plasma and ignition. · $\tau_{\text{conf}}\sim\frac{R}{4c_s},\qquad \text{burn fraction}\approx\frac{\rho R}{\rho R+6\ \text{g cm}^{-2}}$
  - **Laser–plasma interactions** `inertial-and-laser-plasmas/laser-plasma-interactions` — How intense light couples to plasma — collisional and collisionless absorption, parametric instabilities, relativistic self-focusing, ion acceleration and radiation reaction. · $n_c\approx\frac{1.1\times10^{21}}{\lambda[\mu\text{m}]^2}\ \text{cm}^{-3},\qquad T_{\text{hot}}\approx m_ec^2\left(\sqrt{1+a_0^2/2}-1\right)$
  - **Plasma wakefield acceleration** `inertial-and-laser-plasmas/plasma-wakefield-acceleration` — Accelerating particles in plasma waves driven by lasers or particle beams — GV/m–TV/m gradients, bubble regime, beam quality, staging and applications. · $\lambda_p=\frac{2\pi c}{\omega_p}\approx33\ \mu\text{m}\left(\frac{10^{18}}{n_e[\text{cm}^{-3}]}\right)^{1/2},\qquad L_d\approx\frac{\lambda_p^3}{\lambda^2}$
- **Low-temperature and applied plasmas** `low-temperature-and-applied-plasmas` — Weakly ionized, non-equilibrium plasmas used in technology — gas discharges, semiconductor processing, atmospheric-pressure plasmas, plasma medicine and agriculture. · $V_b=\frac{Bpd}{\ln(Apd)-\ln\ln(1+1/\gamma_{se})},\qquad T_e\gg T_i\approx T_{\text{gas}}$
  - **Atmospheric-pressure plasmas and applications** `low-temperature-and-applied-plasmas/atmospheric-pressure-plasmas-and-applications` — Cold plasmas at atmospheric pressure — dielectric-barrier discharges, plasma jets, plasma–liquid interactions, plasma medicine, agriculture and environmental uses. · $e+\text{O}_2\to e+2\text{O},\quad \text{O}+\text{O}_2+M\to\text{O}_3+M$
  - **Plasma discharges and processing** `low-temperature-and-applied-plasmas/plasma-discharges-and-processing` — Gas breakdown and discharge regimes, capacitively and inductively coupled RF plasmas, sheaths, plasma etching and deposition in semiconductor manufacturing. · $\Gamma_i=n_su_B,\ u_B=\sqrt{\frac{k_BT_e}{m_i}}\ (\text{Bohm flux}),\qquad s\approx\lambda_D\left(\frac{2eV_0}{k_BT_e}\right)^{3/4}\ (\text{Child law sheath})$
- **Magnetic confinement fusion** `magnetic-confinement-fusion` — Holding 100-million-degree plasma with magnetic fields — tokamaks, stellarators, turbulent transport, and the path through ITER to burning plasmas and power plants. · $nT\tau_E>3\times10^{21}\ \text{keV s m}^{-3}\ (\text{D–T ignition}),\qquad Q=\frac{P_{\text{fus}}}{P_{\text{heat}}},\qquad \beta=\frac{2\mu_0\langle p\rangle}{B^2}$
  - **Burning plasmas and ITER** `magnetic-confinement-fusion/burning-plasmas-and-iter` — Plasmas heated mainly by their own fusion alphas — ITER's goals, alpha-particle physics, power exhaust, materials, tritium breeding and paths to fusion power plants. · $P_\alpha=\tfrac14n^2\langle\sigma v\rangle E_\alpha V,\qquad \text{Li-6}+n\to\text{T}+{}^4\text{He}+4.8\ \text{MeV}$
  - **Stellarators** `magnetic-confinement-fusion/stellarators` — Currentless toroidal confinement using 3D-shaped coils — rotational transform, neoclassical optimization, quasi-symmetry and Wendelstein 7-X. · $\iota=\frac{1}{q}=\frac{d\psi_p}{d\psi_t},\qquad \text{quasi-symmetry: }|B|=B(\psi,M\theta-N\phi)$
  - **Tokamaks** `magnetic-confinement-fusion/tokamaks` — Toroidal magnetic confinement with a plasma current — equilibrium and safety factor, H-mode and pedestal, ELMs, disruptions, heating and current drive, record discharges. · $q(r)=\frac{rB_\phi}{RB_\theta},\qquad n_G=\frac{I_p}{\pi a^2}\ [10^{20}\,\text{m}^{-3},\ \text{MA},\ \text{m}],\qquad \beta_N=\frac{\beta\,aB_0}{I_p}$
  - **Turbulent transport and gyrokinetics** `magnetic-confinement-fusion/turbulent-transport-and-gyrokinetics` — Why heat leaks out of fusion plasmas — drift-wave turbulence, ion- and electron-temperature-gradient modes, zonal flows, gyrokinetic theory and simulation. · $\chi_{\text{gB}}\sim\frac{\rho_i^2v_{ti}}{L},\qquad \frac{\omega}{\Omega_i}\sim\frac{\rho_i}{L}\sim\frac{\delta f}{f}\ll1\ (\text{gyrokinetic ordering})$
- **Magnetohydrodynamics** `magnetohydrodynamics` — Plasmas and conducting fluids as magnetized fluids — ideal MHD and Alfvén waves, magnetic reconnection, dynamos and MHD turbulence.
  - **Dynamo theory** `magnetohydrodynamics/dynamo-theory` — How flows generate and sustain magnetic fields — kinematic and mean-field dynamos, the α-effect, solar cycle, geodynamo and laboratory dynamos. · $\frac{\partial\bar{\mathbf B}}{\partial t}=\nabla\times(\bar{\mathbf v}\times\bar{\mathbf B}+\alpha\bar{\mathbf B})+(\eta+\beta)\nabla^2\bar{\mathbf B},\qquad R_m=\frac{UL}{\eta}$
  - **Ideal MHD and Alfvén waves** `magnetohydrodynamics/ideal-mhd-and-alfven-waves` — The ideal MHD equations, frozen-in magnetic flux, Alfvén and magnetosonic waves, MHD equilibrium and stability (kink, sausage, interchange). · $\nabla p=\mathbf J\times\mathbf B,\qquad \omega=k_\parallel v_A,\qquad q(a)=\frac{aB_\phi}{RB_\theta}>1\ (\text{Kruskal–Shafranov})$
  - **Magnetic reconnection** `magnetohydrodynamics/magnetic-reconnection` — Breaking and reconnecting magnetic field lines — Sweet–Parker and Petschek models, Hall and collisionless reconnection, plasmoid instability, solar flares and MMS observations. · $\frac{v_{\text{in}}}{v_A}\sim S^{-1/2},\ S=\frac{\mu_0Lv_A}{\eta};\qquad \frac{v_{\text{in}}}{v_A}\sim0.1\ (\text{fast, collisionless})$
  - **MHD turbulence** `magnetohydrodynamics/mhd-turbulence` — Turbulence in magnetized plasmas — Iroshnikov–Kraichnan and Goldreich–Sridhar theories, anisotropic cascades, critical balance, kinetic-scale dissipation and the solar wind. · $\mathbf z^\pm=\mathbf v\pm\frac{\mathbf B}{\sqrt{\mu_0\rho}},\qquad E(k_\perp)\propto k_\perp^{-5/3},\quad k_\parallel\propto k_\perp^{2/3}$
- **Plasma fundamentals** `plasma-fundamentals` — What makes a plasma — Debye shielding and plasma parameters, single-particle motion and drifts, kinetic theory and Landau damping, waves, instabilities and strongly coupled plasmas. · $\lambda_D=\sqrt{\frac{\epsilon_0k_BT_e}{n_ee^2}},\qquad \omega_{pe}=\sqrt{\frac{n_ee^2}{\epsilon_0m_e}},\qquad \Lambda=4\pi n\lambda_D^3\gg1,\qquad \Omega_c=\frac{qB}{m}$
  - **Debye shielding and plasma parameters** `plasma-fundamentals/debye-shielding-and-plasma-parameters` — Quasi-neutrality, Debye screening, plasma frequency, collisionality and the criteria that define a plasma across lab, space and astrophysical regimes. · $\phi(r)=\frac{q}{4\pi\epsilon_0r}e^{-r/\lambda_D},\qquad \eta_{\text{Spitzer}}\approx5\times10^{-5}\frac{Z\ln\Lambda}{T_e[\text{eV}]^{3/2}}\ \Omega\,\text{m}$
  - **Dusty and strongly coupled plasmas** `plasma-fundamentals/dusty-and-strongly-coupled-plasmas` — Plasmas where Coulomb energy rivals thermal energy — dusty-plasma crystals, ultracold neutral plasmas, warm dense matter and one-component plasma models. · $\Gamma=\frac{Z^2e^2}{4\pi\epsilon_0ak_BT},\ a=\left(\frac{3}{4\pi n}\right)^{1/3};\qquad V(r)=\frac{Q^2}{4\pi\epsilon_0r}e^{-r/\lambda_D}$

  - **Single-particle motion and drifts** `plasma-fundamentals/single-particle-motion-and-drifts` — Charged particles in electromagnetic fields — gyration, guiding-centre drifts, adiabatic invariants, magnetic mirrors and radiation belts.

## Curated themes

- [Analogue gravity — horizons in fluids and condensates](connections/themes/analogue-gravity.md) — Sound in a flowing fluid propagates on an effective curved spacetime; where the flow outruns sound there is a horizon that should emit thermal phonons — a laboratory test of the kinematics behind Hawking radiation. *(fields 05, 04, 01, 03)*
- [Black-hole thermodynamics, information and holography](connections/themes/black-hole-thermodynamics-and-holography.md) — Black holes have a temperature and an entropy, which puts G, ħ, c and k_B in one formula and turns gravity into a problem of statistical mechanics and quantum information — culminating in holography, where spacetime geometry encodes entanglement. *(fields 05, 03, 04, 06, 08)*
- [Neutron stars — where six branches meet](connections/themes/neutron-stars-as-a-meeting-point.md) — A neutron star cannot be described by any one branch — general relativity sets its structure, nuclear and particle physics its equation of state, condensed matter its crust and superfluids, plasma physics its magnetosphere, and astrophysics and gravitational waves are how we measure it. *(fields 07, 05, 06, 08, 09, 10)*
- [Pairing across scales — metals, nuclei, neutron stars and cold atoms](connections/themes/pairing-across-scales.md) — BCS pairing is a property of any degenerate Fermi system with an attraction near its Fermi surface; the same gap equation describes superconductors (meV), atomic nuclei and neutron-star matter (MeV) and ultracold Fermi gases (nK). *(fields 08, 07, 04, 09, 03)*
- [Path integrals — quantum amplitudes and partition functions](connections/themes/path-integrals-quantum-and-statistical.md) — Rotating time to imaginary values turns Feynman's sum over histories into a Boltzmann-weighted partition function, so quantum field theory in D dimensions becomes classical statistical mechanics in D dimensions — the basis of lattice QCD, quantum Monte Carlo and instanton physics. *(fields 04, 03, 06, 00)*
- [The renormalization group and universality](connections/themes/renormalization-group-and-universality.md) — One idea for two fields — couplings that run with energy in quantum field theory, and critical exponents shared by unrelated systems in statistical mechanics — joined exactly by the Euclidean field theory ↔ statistical model correspondence. *(fields 03, 06, 08, 07, 00)*
- [Symmetry breaking and the Higgs–Anderson mechanism](connections/themes/symmetry-breaking-and-the-higgs-anderson-mechanism.md) — A superconductor and the electroweak vacuum are the same kind of state — a condensate that hides a gauge symmetry, gives the gauge field a mass and turns the would-be Goldstone mode into its longitudinal polarization. *(fields 00, 03, 06, 08)*
- [Geometric phases, gauge fields and band topology](connections/themes/topology-and-geometric-phases.md) — The Berry phase of a slowly transported quantum state has the mathematics of a gauge field; integrated over a crystal's Brillouin zone it becomes a quantized Chern number — the quantum Hall conductance — and its monopoles are Weyl points. *(fields 04, 08, 02, 06, 01)*
